`taxjson-sum-gains` (each input's parts checked first, a refusal naming the file) and lib/handoff read the Result; form_export, export, split_gains and carryover (step 6c(3)), lib/edge_cases (6c(4)), reconcile_slips (load_computed) and t1135 (join_income_gains) (6c(5)), and wash_radar (the engine's losses: _EngineLosses, _us_engine_losses) and harvest (load_positions, load_inventory_agg, _option_quote_symbols) (6c(6a)), and the cli's sum and estimate (cmd_summary), leaps-sum, ccd-sum, winners and losers (totals), leaps and gains (listings), wash-sales (cmd_wash_sales) and common's _real_world_legs (6c(6b1)), and div-estimate's and option-yield's holdings, _issuer_split and _section_1256_of_docs (summaries), shares and list (positions), _last_loss_by_class (plan), common's _capitalized_pil_shares and income_summary's capitalized_pil_shares (6c(6b2)), the audit (6c(6c1)), and trace_format's trace and report blocks, taxjson-explain's summaries, filed_locks' aggregates_from_gains and the option reports' _option_gains_report (6c(6c2)), and trade_stats (stats: _deemed_gains, economic_pnl, closed_trades), in_kind's contribution_results and pipeline's inventory reads (sanity, stages' _blend_conservation_gaps, inputs' _warn_expired_open_options) (6c(6c3)) read their rows through views over the document's own dicts (`GainRow(e)`, `Holding(item)`: not copies, since the dicts flow on to other commands; a view reads an integer too large for a float as inf, so every list read through one goes through the row funnel, on the rows and fields its views read (`src/taxjson/lib/json_input.py` — `read_rows_doc`): `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_side_doc_or_die` for stats' other documents (`src/taxjson/lib/trade_stats.py` — `side_row`, `SIDE_FIELDS`), and `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_checked_gains_docs`, `_blend_gap_messages`; or the reader checks each figure is finite itself: sanity's holdings, the expired options). missing_history's warned losses are engine reads: in memory, inside the engine; the other gains readers move to it one at a time (step 6c).
- `src/taxjson/model/evidence.py` — `parse_broker_lots`, `format_broker_lots`, `broker_lot`, `parse_commission_refunds`, `format_commission_refunds`, `commission_refund`, `split_cut_legs`, `parse_cut_leg`, `parse_cut_legs`, `format_cut_legs`, `cut_leg`, `cut_leg_symbol`, `cut_leg_action`, `UNKNOWN_OPENING_LEG`, `add_broker_lot`, `add_commission_refund`, `join_cut_legs`, `BrokerLot`, `CommissionRefund`, `CutLeg`, `EvidenceError`, `BROKER_LOTS_INVALID`, `parse_corp_cash`, `format_corp_cash`, `corp_cash`, `corp_cash_tt`, `corp_cash_of_tt`, `CorpCash`, `parse_settle_native`, `format_settle_native`, `settle_native`, `stamp_settle_rate`, `settle_leg`, `settle_native_tt`, `settle_native_of_tt`, `SettleNative`: the evidence five row fields pack into a string (`broker_lots`, `commission_refunds`, `opening_cut_legs`, `corp_cash`, `settle_native`), as frozen records whose numbers are the Decimals of their tokens, and back in each writer's spelling. The writers (`src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/ib_extractor.py` `_closed_lot`, `_ib_fold_refund_into`; `src/taxjson/lib/opening.py` `_note_cut_legs`) and the readers (`src/taxjson/lib/purchase_drafts.py` `broker_lot_floats`, `src/taxjson/lib/fx_cash_v2.py` `_refund_legs`, `src/taxjson/engine/premium_ledger.py` `_AssignPremiumLedger`) go through it: a writer adds to a field with `add_broker_lot`, `add_commission_refund` or `join_cut_legs`, keeping what it held as written. Each parser keeps its reader's rules, but a cut leg is read whole where the ledger uses it (step 6b). The corp cash's writer (`src/taxjson/lib/corp_actions.py` `_corp_cash`) and readers (`src/taxjson/lib/cash_legs.py` `_corp_cash_parts`; `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_fx_cash.py` `_cash_by_day`), the settlement facts' writers and readers (`src/taxjson/lib/futures.py` `_part`, `stamp_rate`, `engine_pool_key`, `pool_across_accounts`, `opposite_broker_positions`) and the two fields' .tt tokens (`src/taxjson/lib/tt_format.py` `_check_corp`, `_corp_tokens`, `_check_futures_settlement`, `_trade_tokens`) go through it too,. Over the rows the golden project books' runs write in work/, each packed field reads back to itself through its codec (`tests/test_model_evidence_books.py`).

## The gains engines

`src/taxjson/engine/` holds both engines (step 10; lib/core.py, the shim
that re-exported their names and the transaction model's, went after
v1.0.0). The Canadian engine
pools shares at adjusted cost base per security across taxable accounts and
applies the superficial-loss rule (with sheltered and affiliated accounts in
view). The US engine (experimental) keeps FIFO lots, per account when asked,
and applies the wash-sale rule. Both handle splits, renames, option exercise
and assignment, and option replacement.

The package, one module per concern (`scripts/engine-table.txt` says what
moved where). Shared: `src/taxjson/engine/consts.py` (the money constants, and how a
trace shows a figure), `src/taxjson/engine/journal.py` (a listing journal's legs and
currency factor, an opening's source), `src/taxjson/engine/assign.py` (each option
ASSIGN paired with its own stock legs), `src/taxjson/engine/rows.py` (the one input
contract, row ids, currencies and dates), `src/taxjson/engine/splits.py` (repeated
splits dropped, rename ratios folded), `src/taxjson/engine/premium_ledger.py` (the
premiums an assignment stages), `src/taxjson/engine/replacement.py` (the warn-only
replacement checks), `src/taxjson/engine/taxrules.py` (the base class) and
`src/taxjson/engine/by_country.py` (the engine for a country, the one module importing
both). Canada, `src/taxjson/engine/ca/`: the pools, the run's helpers, the indexes,
own moves, the marked-short router, the loop's records, the denial pass,
the walk, the fold, the solve and `CanadaTaxRules`. The US, `src/taxjson/engine/us/`:
the lots and their store, the run, its terms, the wash-sale rule, the option
scan, the preamble, the trades, the walk and `USATaxRules`. `ca/` and `us/`
import nothing from each other.

- `src/taxjson/lib/tx_load.py` — `load_transactions`, `read_book_rows`, `coerce_transaction_row`, `canonical_date` (a book read into rows; a blank `id` read as none, backlog TJ-5041); `src/taxjson/lib/option_symbols.py` — `is_option_symbol`; `src/taxjson/lib/contract_size.py` — `equity_option_size`, `option_contract_size` (a contract's size: the MULT line, the row's declared size, the assumed 100); `src/taxjson/lib/row_types.py` — `IN_KIND_CONTRIBUTION_TYPE` (the row kinds); `src/taxjson/model/transaction.py` — `TaxTransaction` (the model's, see the model section above); `src/taxjson/engine/rows.py` — `require_computable_rows`, `SYMBOL_REQUIRED_ACTIONS`, `carry_row_marks`; `src/taxjson/engine/taxrules.py` — `TaxRules` (`country`, and the stages both engines share: `_dedupe_splits`, `_emit_replacement_warnings`, `_stamp_overrides`); `src/taxjson/engine/ca/taxrules.py` — `CanadaTaxRules` (`compute_gains` is three stages since step 4o: `_prepare`, the preamble that returns the run and the empty books, with its one pass over the SPLIT rows (`src/taxjson/engine/ca/run_helpers.py` `_ca_split_targets`: the option lineages and the split symbols); the solve, the loop; `_finish`, the report); `src/taxjson/engine/ca/run_helpers.py` — `_ca_ev_key`, `_ca_write_year`, `_ca_grant_applies`, `_ca_short_lot_open`, `_ca_open_short_option`, `_ca_opt_root`, `_ca_row_pool_key` (the run's module helpers: they take `run` and read their inputs from it, step 4r), `_ca_walk_date`, `_ca_stock_short_key`, `_ca_tl_after` (the iteration's: `run`, then `it`), `_sl_holder`, `_sl_holder_rank`, `_sl_pkey`, `_sl_call_und`, `_sl_symbol_asof`, `_sl_opening_qty`, `_sl_wpos` (the denial pass's, over `pas` and `run` or a fact handed to them); `src/taxjson/engine/ca/denial.py` — `_sl_class_balances`; `src/taxjson/engine/ca/solve.py` — `_ca_solve` (the loop); `src/taxjson/engine/us/taxrules.py` — `USATaxRules` (three stages since step 4p: `_prepare`, the preamble that returns the run; the walk; `_finish`, the report; the engine's helpers below take `run` first and read their inputs from it since step 4r, but three the pre-run scan and the timeline recorder share stay pure over one keyword input the walk hands from the run, and the pre-run consumers' own partials stay local: `_row_after`, `_nkey`, `_us_state`; the planner `_plan_paid_compensation`'s building run is the prepare, the walk and the payments plan alone since step 4q); `src/taxjson/engine/us/prepare.py` — `_us_scan_events` (the replacement scan over the book's events), `_us_symbol_currencies` and `_us_disposition_groups` (out of the preamble), `_us_dividend_entries` (the report's dividend and payment-in-lieu entries, the compensation paid on shorts capitalised where the plan says), `_us_plan_payments` (writes that plan over the payment rows); `src/taxjson/engine/us/walk.py` — `_us_walk_rows` (the walk), `_us_book_journal`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/wash.py` — `_us_note`, `_us_ix`, `_us_set_ref`, `_us_rep_key`, `_us_split_covers`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/lots.py` — `_us_plan_disposition`, `_us_draw_long_lots`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/lot_store.py` — `_us_insert_lots`, `_us_lots_copy`, `_us_ikey`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/trades.py` — `_us_gain_entry`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/wash.py` — `_us_find_replacements_in_window`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/option_scan.py` — `detect_option_replacement_matches`; `src/taxjson/engine/premium_ledger.py` — `_AssignPremiumLedger` (`_in_snapshot`: an exercise whose share leg an OPENING snapshot replaced stages no premium, backlog TJ-0962); `src/taxjson/engine/assign.py` — `_pair_assign_legs`, `_assign_candidates`, `_allocate_assign_legs` (`_run_passes`, the competition; `_compete` and `_fill_ties`, the Canadian engine's two stages: the competition with each tie group as one option, then the groups filling their needs in a placement-free order), `_pro_rata` and `_series_rank` (each assignment's own stock legs by identity, the one allocator the stats views' pairing in lib/trade_stats calls too; with `tie_pro_rata` a leg equal strikes of one day cannot tell apart is theirs pro rata by what each delivers, in whole shares, TJ-2750); `src/taxjson/engine/rows.py` — `not_a_purchase`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/terms.py` — `held_more_than_one_year`; `src/taxjson/engine/by_country.py` — `get_tax_rules`: the engine for a country; the transaction model's use (every JSON row goes through tx_load's coercion: types, finite numbers, dates written YYYY-MM-DD); the computation's input contract (`src/taxjson/engine/rows.py` `require_computable_rows`, called by both engines' `compute_gains` and by `src/taxjson/lib/pipeline.py` — `prepare_books`: a trade without its amount, an empty date or symbol, an action the engines do not book); the two engines' shells (`src/taxjson/engine/ca/taxrules.py` `CanadaTaxRules`, then its `compute_gains`; the row-by-row logic is in the walk and the denial pass, two entries below, and in the US engine's walk); the US lot and wash-sale machinery's indexes (`src/taxjson/engine/us/run.py`), the option replacement checks (`src/taxjson/engine/replacement.py`), the assignment premiums (`src/taxjson/engine/premium_ledger.py`) and the assignments' pairing (`src/taxjson/engine/assign.py`). The rows of one sale for Canada's superficial-loss formula (a day's uninterrupted fills, CA-SL-08), which the engines, the loss overrides and the wash radar read: `src/taxjson/lib/sale_groups.py` — `disposition_groups`.
- `src/taxjson/lib/row_types.py` — `is_stock_dividend`; `src/taxjson/lib/pool_eps.py` — `pool_qty_eps`; `src/taxjson/engine/splits.py` — `_rename_ratio_groups`, `_multi_ratio_rename_groups`, `_fold_per_account_rename_ratios`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/terms.py` — `_per_account_rename_ratios`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/wash.py` — `_us_split_covers`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/walk.py` — `_ent_date`: a merger delivered at different whole-share ratios per account (Canada: folded into one event at the holdings-weighted ratio, CA-CORP-13, the weights moved by the own-move legs the engine books none of (`src/taxjson/engine/splits.py` `moves`, backlog TJ-0280); US with per-account lots: each row scales and migrates its own account's lots, US-CORP-14; US symbol-wide lots: folded); the flat tolerance of a pool (shares 1e-6, a coin only float noise) that the ADJUST landing and the `src/taxjson/engine/ca/pool.py` `pending_wash` recovery share with the trades; the §307 stock-dividend spread over the lots entitled to it (bought before the ex / record date).
- `src/taxjson/engine/ca/walk.py` — `_walk_rows` (the walk over one iteration's rows: pools, gains, losses, traces, the timeline; `run`, the invariant state, and `it`, the iteration's; its three action branches as functions, step 4m: `_walk_adjust`, an ADJUST row against the pool, `_walk_split`, a SPLIT row and a rename's pool, `_walk_trade`, a trade row's opening or closing against the pool (`_past_position`: the part of a trade past the position at or under the quantity tolerance, its money kept with the disposition when it is rounding, booked as a position the other way when it is material); `_YearEndPools`, the pools at the end of the project year read as the walk passes it: one copy before the first row past the year end, and on a trade-date year end the pools one by one up to the last row traded in the year); `src/taxjson/engine/ca/denial.py` — `_superficial_loss_pass` (the denial pass of one iteration of the fixed-point loop, `run`, `it` and the iteration's losses and positions: its indexes and helpers, then the loop over the iteration's losses, each loss in four parts), `_sl_trigger_scan` (the loss's window and the purchases and calls in it; returns the loss's `_SLLoss`), `_sl_window_walk` (what each holder still owns of the class at the end of day 30; returns the loss's `_SLHeld`), `_sl_pre_exists` (the phase boundary: whether a row pre-exists its sort date, an opening balance in a symbol a SPLIT placed on that day created excepted), `_sl_row_loss_units`, `_sl_call_units`, `_sl_avail_native`, `_sl_gone_by_end` (the walk's unit helpers over a loss's objects and its two sort-date facts), `_sl_allocate` (the sale's formula over its fills, the units allocated over the triggers, the DISALLOW / ADJUST rows; `_sl_sale_alloc` and `_sl_trigger_holder`: the sale's denied units over its triggers, once per sale, each fill taking its units' share, a trigger sold out before day 30 no replacement (`_sl_sold_out`, over `_sl_pool_rows`: a pool's position changes in the window with the walk's units after each, cached on `_SLIndexes.pool_rows`); `_sl_mk_adjust`: the addition's ADJUST row, placed by `_sl_landing_row` where the position held at day 30 begins when the pool holds no long position in between, from the walk's pool snapshots), `_sl_window_trace` (the denied loss's window for the report: reporting only), `_sl_class_balances`, `_sl_trg`, `_sl_call`, `_sl_sym`; `src/taxjson/engine/ca/holders.py` — `_SLIndexes` (the pass's four indexes over the book, built by the first pass's setup and kept across the loop's iterations), `_CanadaRun` (what the fixed-point loop reads but never rebinds, built once before it: what the pass, the walk, the solve and the finish take as their run parameter), `_CanadaBooks` (what the loop leaves for the report: the converged pass's gains, wash sales, pools, timeline, the solver's verdict, the last iteration's rows and premium ledger; the solve builds and returns it), `_CanadaIteration` (what the loop binds each iteration for the walk, built right before it: what the walk and the pass take as their it parameter), `_SLPass` (what the pass's setup binds for its parts, built once per pass), `_SLLoss` (one loss as the scan establishes it), `_SLHeld` (what the walk finds for it); `src/taxjson/engine/ca/solve.py` — `_ca_solve` (the fixed-point loop itself, step 4o: each iteration's rows, walk and denial pass until a pass finds no new denial; fills the books), `_dis_by_id`; `src/taxjson/engine/ca/fold.py` — `_WalkFold` (the forward denial pass, recommendation 10 of the 2026-11-07 re-extraction: the walk as a re-fold of the affected suffix; `restore` finds the cut, `_cut`, the first row the last pass changed, and installs the last checkpoint at or before it, `_install`; the walk `save`s a checkpoint before each of its `marks`; `fold_state` and `restore` on the premium ledger, the marked-short router and the cost timeline's recorder (`src/taxjson/engine/premium_ledger.py` `_AssignPremiumLedger`, `src/taxjson/engine/ca/router.py` `_MarkedShortRouter`, `src/taxjson/lib/cost_timeline.py` `Recorder`) are the parts of the state those objects own; `tests/test_engine_solver.py` pins the fold against the whole-book walk); `src/taxjson/engine/ca/run_helpers.py` — `_ca_sort_date`, `_ca_trade_money`, `_ca_ev_key`, `_ca_short_lot_open`, `_ca_open_short_option`, `_ca_opt_root`, `_ca_row_pool_key`, `_ca_walk_date`, `_ca_stock_short_key`, `_ca_tl_after`, `_ca_write_year`, `_ca_grant_applies` (the Canadian engine's helpers as module functions; since step 4r the engine's helpers take `run` or `it` and read their inputs from them; the holders carry data alone); `src/taxjson/engine/rows.py` — `_iso_ordinal`; `src/taxjson/engine/ca/indexes.py` — `_DatedRows`, `_ClassBalances`, `_WashAdjustIndex`: the Canadian superficial-loss scans' indexes (each identity class's purchases and calls by date window, its day-30 balances as running sums per holder, the solver's denials and ADJUSTs by loss id); each hands a scan the rows the walk of the whole book kept, in its order, so a run grows with its rows, not loss sales x rows (TJ-0105).
- `src/taxjson/engine/ca/pool.py` — `AcbPool`, `new`, `PoolBook`, `ensure`, `rename`: the Canadian pools' owner (PLAN step 13). A pool is made by `AcbPool.new` alone and the walk's pools change keys through the book (`ensure`, `rename`, and the dict's own pop); its operations: `open` (a purchase or a short's opening sale, with what rides in on it) and `take_pending` (a denied loss's addition parked on the pool joins the first row that leaves it long, never a short: the owner's ruling on TJ-4258; `denied_in_cost`, the tally less what is parked, is what a disposition or a move releases), `add_cost`, `park`, `tally_denied`, `give_up`, `cut_for_roc`, `restore_roc`, `reset_negative` and `late_premium` (an ADJUST row's effects: a cost adjustment, a denied loss's addition and its tally, a return of capital and its reversal), `move_in`, `move_out` (its two cost spellings: an own move's and a journal's), `move_out_lots`, `move_in_from` and `move_beyond` (units that arrive or leave with no sale: a move between the person's accounts, a listing journal), `close` (a sale's units out at the cost the walk computed, the denied losses in the ACB released in their share), `open_lot`, `close_lots` and `retag_lots` (a short option pool's written lots: written, closed first-written first within the closing account, moved between accounts), `leftover` (the part of a trade past zero: a new position, nothing of the closed one), `split` (the units and a short option pool's written lots re-denominated, the cost as it was), `merge` (a rename's source pool into the pool of the new ticker), `drain` (a row left no position, no units or fewer than the tolerance costing under half a cent, whichever row it is: no units, no start date, no lots, no unknown-cost units, `untaint`), `taint`, `taint_from` and `add_sources` (units of unknown cost and their causes), `add_wash_cut` (what a return of capital took of the denied losses, `cut_late`), `stamp_currency`. The pool's fields are read-only attributes over private slots: nothing outside the module writes one, and the copies the reports read are the pool's own (`snapshot`, the year end's; `part`, one account's part of a short; `netted`); a written lot's grant records are set through it too (`record_grant`, `record_grant_loss`). `tests/features/engines/test_pool_owner.py` holds this: no dict writes on the class, read-only fields, no private slot or pool-shaped dict named outside the module, the public methods listed. The book also holds what a spin-off's parent gives up of its denied losses until the new shares arrive (`is_spinoff`, `give_up_to_child`, `child_opens`). `src/taxjson/engine/ca/fold.py` — `_WalkFold`, `save`, `_install`: a checkpoint is the book's own copy (`PoolBook.save`, `restore`: the pools and the spin-off children that are its pools) inside the fold's one deep copy, so a restored child is still the book's pool (`tests/features/engines/test_pool_owner.py`).
- `src/taxjson/engine/us/walk.py` — `_us_walk_rows` (the US engine's walk over the taxable rows: FIFO lots, gains, wash sales, the timeline; `run`, the engine's state, and the two lots it returns; its per-action blocks as functions, step 4n: `_us_walk_lot_move`, `_us_walk_split`, `_us_walk_adjust`, `_us_walk_stock_dividend` (with `_us_allot_exchanged_shares`, the new shares paid on entitled shares a §356 exchange took before the pay date, backlog TJ-4987, and `_us_stock_dividend_owed`, a stock dividend while short: the short lots grow, US-STKDIV-06, backlog TJ-5006; the wash-sale pre-pass reads the row the same way, `src/taxjson/engine/us/prepare.py` — `_us_stkdiv_units`; Canada: `src/taxjson/engine/ca/taxrules.py` — `_stock_dividends_owed` walks each account's rows alone once first (the receiving account's own position decides) and hands every pass the row as the short's units, `src/taxjson/engine/ca/walk.py` — `_ca_stock_dividend_owed`, `STOCK_DIVIDENDS_OWED`, and `src/taxjson/lib/pipeline.py` — `_gains_with_stock_dividend_lines`, `_ca_stock_dividend_cost_lines` drop the declared-amount line for it), `_us_walk_spinoff`, `_us_walk_reorg`, the corporate rows against the lots, and the buy and the sale (`src/taxjson/engine/us/trades.py` — `_us_walk_buy`, a buy's cover of the short lots and its long lot, `_us_walk_sell`, a sale's close against the long lots and its short lot, both writing one realized-gain entry with the row's names, `_us_gain_entry`); the lots at the year end and at `lots_at`, read as the walk passes the day: `src/taxjson/engine/us/lots.py` — `_USDayLots`, `_us_row_after`, one copy before the first row after the day, and on settlement dates the lots pool by pool up to the last row dated and settled by it; a position whose own rows cross the two date orders is an ATTENTION line, `src/taxjson/lib/stage_msg.py` — `YEAR_END_INEXACT`, which `tjs run --strict` stops on, `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_check_year_end_exact`, and the checklist's run step lists, `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `_year_end_inexact`), and the engine's helpers as `_us_*` module functions (`_us_carry_lots`, `_us_book_journal`, ...: since step 4r these helpers take `run` first and read their inputs from it; the pre-run replacement walk's and the timeline recorder's stay partials over locals); `src/taxjson/engine/us/lot_store.py` — `_us_insert_lots`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/lots.py` — `_us_draw_long_lots`, `_us_plan_draw`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/run.py` — `_USRun` (what the engine binds before the walk, built as its prepare stage ends; the walk, its parts and the report stage read it), `_RepIndex`, `_rep_ix`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/wash.py` — `_us_find_replacements_in_window`, `_us_ix`, `_us_set_ref`: the US engine's index of each class's wash-sale replacement records (`long_replacements`, `short_replacements`) by trade-date window (`_us_find_replacements_in_window`, `_us_sold_replacements_in_window`), by row id (the buy's or short's own record) and by the lot each points at (`lot_ref` / `short_lot_ref`, set only through `_us_set_ref`); the scans read the records the walk kept, in its order (TJ-0238).
- `src/taxjson/lib/numeric.py` — `nonneg_float_arg`, `positive_float_arg`; `src/taxjson/model/money.py` — `D`, `round_half_up`, `round_floats`: the argparse number types; the rounding, which lib/numeric re-exports from model/money.
- `src/taxjson/lib/loss_override_note.py` — `parse_line` (the ALLOWLOSS line); `src/taxjson/lib/loss_overrides.py` — `read_project`, `write_state`, `read_state`, `load_file`, `_entry_qty` (a given quantity a finite number above 0, backlog TJ-4999), `flags`, `plan`, `summarize`, `stamp_entries` (each gain row its own share of the denial: `row_share`, what the US walk leaves on a lot's row, `src/taxjson/engine/us/lot_store.py` — `_us_ov_share`, `_us_ov_take`; a Canadian row is one per fill), `gather`, `problems`, `spelling_hint` (a no-match line: how the books spell the sale — another listing of the root, a ticker.map spelling, a date a few days off), `map_view`, `describe`, `warning_message`, `positions`, `STATE`: filing positions against the loss rule (`.tt` ALLOWLOSS lines, CA-SL-18 / US-WASH-25): the line, the run's `work/loss_overrides.json` and the `--loss-overrides` flag every engine run of the books gets (`src/taxjson/lib/pipeline.py` — `add_loss_override_args`, `loss_overrides_from_args`, `GainsRequest`), the sale each line names (`plan`, in the engine's order), what the rule would have denied (the engines record it, `loss_overrides` in a gains file, `loss_override` on the sale's rows), and the views: the run's Warning and its stop (`src/taxjson/pipeline/inputs.py` — `_read_loss_overrides`, `_loss_override_flags`, `_say_loss_overrides`), `sum` and `wash-sales` (FILING POSITIONS), the checklist (`src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `d_filing_positions`); the per-row note (`src/taxjson/lib/loss_override_note.py` — `note_text`) on the return forms (`src/taxjson/lib/tax_forms.py` — `_filing_position`, `_merge_positions`), in `tjs audit` (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_audit.py` — `build_event`) and the explain traces (`src/taxjson/lib/trace_format.py` — `filing_position_text`), the US radar (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_wash_radar.py` — `_us_engine_losses`), `tjs carryover` (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_carryover.py` — `filing_positions`, `_render_notes`) and `tjs handoff` (`src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `_handoff_positions`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/wash_scope.py` — `scope_note`, `scope_lines`, `advisory_lines`, `scope_short`, `scope_more`: what the planning verdicts can and cannot see, per country.
- `src/taxjson/engine/rows.py` — `RuleTable`, `override_record`, `closed_record`, `retest_record`, `in_kind_record`: the rule each decision record names (PLAN step 11), {decided row id: {record id: rule id}}, which compute_gains returns under its rules key when it is not empty; a filing position's record, a replacement closed before the loss, a US retest beside the row's own disallowance and an in-kind contribution's sale are keyed apart from the record their row also holds; one field per run (`src/taxjson/engine/ca/holders.py` — `_CanadaRun`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/run.py` — `_USRun`).
- `src/taxjson/engine/ca/denial.py` — `_sl_allocate`, `_sl_mk_adjust`, `_ca_live_rules`: the Canadian records' rules. The denial pass notes each DISALLOW (its loss entry's rule: `src/taxjson/engine/ca/walk.py` — `_walk_trade`; `src/taxjson/engine/ca/run_helpers.py` — `_ca_open_short_option`), override and ADJUST as it makes it (an addition that waits for the position held at day 30, moved or not, CA-SL-19: `_sl_sold_out`; an affiliated person's or a registered account's addition, CA-SL-09, the acquirer's); an addition still parked when the walk ends is a Warning (`src/taxjson/engine/ca/pools.py` — `_warn_waiting_additions`); the finish keeps those of the converged books' records (a withdrawn denial names nothing) and adds the walk's own gain records' (`src/taxjson/engine/ca/walk.py` — `_ca_deemed_gain`, and the in-kind contribution's record).
- `src/taxjson/engine/us/wash.py` — `_us_note_rules`: the US wash records' rules, noted as each is made (a sale's, a cover's or a retest's, `retest`: US-WASH-22, under `retest_record` beside the row's own, and each replacement's: a replacement short's proceeds cut, US-WASH-27); a filing position's would-be disallowance names its own (`src/taxjson/engine/us/lot_store.py` — `_us_ov_take`), and so do the walk's deemed gain and §356 boot (`src/taxjson/engine/us/walk.py` — `_us_walk_adjust`, `_us_walk_reorg`). A lot whose shares replaced a loss says so itself (`_US_LOT_USED`, set where a match adds to it and by a §355 spin-off on a parent lot holding a deferral), and `_us_record_left_shares` reads it: shares already used never replace a second loss, wherever a spin-off moved their basis (backlog TJ-4986).
- `src/taxjson/characterize/foreign_roc.py` — `NoteRule`, `foreign_roc_rule`, `characterize_foreign_roc`: a characterisation tells its caller each row's rule through a callback (taxjson-brokerage passes none).
- `src/taxjson/lib/pipeline.py` — `run_gains`: pops the engines' rule table after the trace sink, so no gains document carries it; the trace file's superficial-loss and wash-sale blocks end with the rules their records name (`src/taxjson/lib/trace_format.py` — `_rules_line`; `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_gains.py` — `write_traces_file`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/tax_logic.py` — `ENGINE_RULES`: the rules a record can name; a test asserts one by its constant (`tests/_support/engine_rules.py` — `assert_rule`, `assert_noted`, `rules_of`: any other rule is refused), and check 12 counts one as cited only where engine or characterize code names it (`scripts/check_tax_rules.py` — `engine_named`); a test asserts each on a record (`tests/test_rule_ids.py` — `_asserted`).

## The gains run and the wash passes

`pipeline.py` is the one definition of a gains run: load-side preparation
(transfers, missing-history openings), the country's engine options (option
premium timing, US per-account lots), then the engine. `taxjson-gains` is its
command line. After each account has gains, a second pass re-runs them with
the other accounts in view (cross-account superficial loss); in Canada the
taxable accounts run as one blended pass and are split back per account.

- `src/taxjson/lib/pipeline.py` — `run_gains`, `GainsRequest`, `prepare_books`, `engine_options`, `option_timing_from_settings`, `option_timing_flags`, `place_retro_wash_adjustments`, `annotate_inventory_multipliers`, `declared_multipliers`, `tt_json_path`: one gains run; US retroactive wash adjustments and contract multipliers.
- `src/taxjson/lib/cost_timeline.py` — `Recorder`, `touch`, `sweep`, `finish`, `fold_state`, `restore`, `rows`, `row_ids`, `row_seqs`, `check`, `pools_on`, `inventory_on`, `short_rows_on`, `VERSION`, `FIELDS`: the engines' cost timeline (backlog TJ-0417), each pool's quantity and cost after every row that changed it, dated on the engine's basis (Canada's pooled, account ""; the US engine's per account); asked for with `GainsRequest.cost_timeline` (`taxjson-gains --cost-timeline FILE`, `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_gains.py` — `cost_timeline`), recorded by `src/taxjson/engine/ca/pools.py` — `_ca_pool_state`, `_ca_touched`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/lots.py` — `_us_tl_state`, `_us_tl_held`; read by `tjs t1135` (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_t1135.py` — `walk_costs`), the year-end snapshot (`src/taxjson/lib/handoff.py` — `snapshot`) and `tjs list --date` (`src/taxjson/cli/positions.py` — `_dated_inventory`); the run writes one beside each gains pass (`src/taxjson/pipeline/freshness.py` — `timeline_beside`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `stage_account`, `stage_wash_pass`, `stage_blended_wash_pass`).
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_gains.py` — `main`, `_request`, `_suggest_missing_history_and_exit`, `write_traces_file`, `_since_shown` (a block of the traces file shows the pool lines since the block above of the same pool): the `taxjson-gains` tool. A sale's trace is a `src/taxjson/lib/trace_format.py` — `SharedTrace`: a view of its pool's running trace in `src/taxjson/engine/ca/walk.py` (`symbol_acb_traces`), read as the whole history by `tjs audit` and taxjson-explain, never copied per sale.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_split_gains.py` — `split_for_account`, `main`: splits a blended gains run back into per-account files (an account's share of a pool walked with the splits another account of the pool reports: `src/taxjson/lib/positions_check.py` — `pool_splits`, `blended_pool_rows`, `book_rows`, which `sanity` and `list DATE` read too).
- `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `stage_wash_pass`, `stage_blended_wash_pass`, `_blend_conservation_gaps`, `_blend_gap_messages` (its files' inventory quantities through the row checks), `_checked_gains_docs` (each account's gains document, its in-kind contribution rows' `_IN_KIND_FIELDS` through the row checks; a refused one left out, said with the in-kind message: for the in-kind results), `_wash_flags`, `_render_wash_outputs`, `_record_wash_inputs`: the wash passes in a run, and the record of what each wash file was built from (`work/.wash_inputs.json`, for the stale check).
- `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_flag_undated_listings`, `_undated_listing_rows`, `_books_did`, `_unapplied`, `_map_renames`, `_one_pool`, `flag_undated_account`, `undated_strict`, `LISTING_EVIDENCE_SUFFIX`; `src/taxjson/lib/stage_msg.py` — `undated_listing_note`, `parse_undated_listing_note`, `UNDATED_LISTING`, `UNSHOWN_LISTING`, `MOVED_LISTING`, `UNDATED_LISTING_GAIN`; `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `_undated_listing`; `src/taxjson/lib/first_run.py` — `parser_warnings`; `src/taxjson/lib/out.py` — `ATTENTION_TOPICS`: an IB cost row (a return of capital) left on a listing no dated row of its statement shows held that day, the account's exports naming another listing of the ticker (the parser's notes: `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/ib_extractor.py` — `_ib_finish_income`; the split exports, a statement trading one listing later, a statement with no Open Positions): after each account's split (under `run --account`, from the account's own gains), an ATTENTION per row, the payment and the listing it may belong to first, then what the books did with it from the engine's cost timeline (the cost reduced, the part booked as a gain, or not applied), the remedy last; none where the map books the listings as one pool; kept in `work/<account>_listing_evidence.diag` for the wash twin's banner, the run's closing list and the checklist's run step; `run --strict` stops, naming the rows (backlog TJ-5011, TJ-5201, TJ-5202; the dated replay across exports is TJ-0669, open). The closing list's short form: `src/taxjson/lib/first_run.py` — `_undated_concise`. A cost row moves onto a listing only another statement of the broker account names when that statement's rows of it leave room for a holding on the row's date: `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/ib_extractor.py` — `_ib_note_held` (each statement's listings with their first dated row, `held_by_file`), `_ib_held_elsewhere`, `_ib_could_predate`, `_ib_listings_named`. One broker income row two overlapping statements put on two listings is one row: `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/ib_extractor.py` — `_ib_cross_income_listings`, `_ib_income_key`, `listed_income`.

## Per-account reports

Each account ends with a `reports/<name>.sum` text report and its JSON twin:
gains, income, option summaries and holdings. Cross-account reports (covered
calls, long options, the wash radar, cross-listing lint) and the fee report
come after.

- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_sum_gains.py` — `format_report`, `_ticker_rows`, `_consolidated_totals`, `load_gains_data`, `main`: the gains section of a `.sum` (per currency, the ticker rows and the CONSOLIDATED TOTALS block in those two helpers); its aggregates, `src/taxjson/lib/gain_summary.py` — `summarize_gains`, from a model/result `Result` (or the JSON object it is made from).
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_sum_income.py` — `format_report`, `load_income_data`, `_checked_amount`, `main`; `src/taxjson/lib/income_summary.py` — `summarize_income`, `income_amount` (the amount a row counts: a dividend's gross, else the net; the hand-off's Dec-31 rows record it too): the income section.
- `src/taxjson/bin/_option_gains_report.py` — `process_data`, `load_inputs`, `main`: shared body of the two option reports.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_ccd_gains.py` — `process_data`, `main`: short calls (covered calls) by underlying.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_leaps_gains.py` — `process_data`, `main`: long options by underlying.
- `src/taxjson/lib/listing_journals.py` — `book_journals`, `evidenced_journals`, `identity_find`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/inputs.py` — `_raw_book_journals`, `_account_sidecars`, `_raw_rate_lookup`; `src/taxjson/engine/journal.py` — `_journal_legs`, `_journal_factor`; `src/taxjson/engine/ca/own_moves.py` — `_journal_units`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/walk.py` — `_us_book_journal`, `_us_carry_lots`; `src/taxjson/lib/row_types.py` — `LISTING_JOURNAL_TYPE`; `src/taxjson/lib/pipeline.py` — `_handle_transfers`: a journal between two listings in the native-currency books behind the holdings export (backlog TJ-0361, TJ-0363): the run pairs the account's own transfer legs and its transfer evidence within the map's identity classes and writes two listing-journal TRANSFER rows into work/<account>_raw.json on the journal's date; both engines move the units with their cost (a short the other way), restated in the new listing's currency by the in-leg's rate; the transfer handling sets the legs aside, and the run passes the export no evidence.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_export.py` — `render_report`, `render_holdings_toml` (`--crypto`: a crypto account's own books; `--symbol-suffix`: the exchange's venue suffix on each coin, its dust named in a comment; `--venue-book`, `_venue_suffixes`: an account of several exchanges, each coin its own venue's; a holding whose cost the books do not have: `cost_unknown`, `_cost_unknown_note`, the moves of your own that brought it `_own_move_arrivals`; its cost after the passes across accounts, `--blended-gains`, `_blended_fill`, `_blended_note`, `cost_source`, written by `src/taxjson/pipeline/diagnostics.py` — `fill_moved_cost` after the blended passes, the holdings row asked again: `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `stage_moved_holdings`, backlog TJ-0598); `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_export.py` — `process_data_report`, `contract_multiplier_of` (an option's declared size; unknown when the inputs disagree), `_holdings_toml_to_inventory` (a holdings TOML read back: its cost as the positions reader takes it, mixed-currency buckets, `asset_type`, base-currency and blended figures, its `as_of`, a `holdings_count` checked; `dispatch_toml` applies the map's GLOBAL renames to it), `_load_trade_events` (each holding's current-position `trades`, replayed in the engine's order: `_replay_key`, `src/taxjson/lib/country.py` — `engine_sort_profile`), `_evidence_rows`, `_evidenced_journals`, `_apply_transfer_evidence`, `_replay_moves_on_base` (the transfer sidecars' journals between listings, applied to the holdings), `main`: holdings as a text report or a TOML snapshot.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_fees.py` — `aggregate` (`trade_value`, the %NOTNL denominator), `metrics`, `render_text`, `render_json`, `main`: the fee report by broker (`taxjson-fees-sum`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/staking_report.py` — `build_report`, `render`: reward evidence, commissions, recorded income and acquisition cost, grouped by exchange and coin with unknown-evidence coverage; `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_staking_rows`, `_staking_view`; `src/taxjson/cli/listings.py` — `cmd_staking`; `src/taxjson/cli/totals.py` — `cmd_staking_sum`: period/account/exchange/coin selection from the final base-currency books and the two command views.
- `src/taxjson/lib/report_model.py` — `resolve_gains_files`, `stale_wash_inputs`, `_STALE_SAID` (each stale-wash warning once per process), `record_wash_inputs`, `_book_accounts` (the roster of accounts a pass saw: a new one makes it stale), `format_report_table`, `load_report_json`: shared report pieces; which gains file a report reads and whether the wash-adjusted one is stale (its own books, and every member of its cross-account pass, from the `wash` field of the wash file's record: `WASH_FIELD`, `_wash_record`; TJ-1804).
- `src/taxjson/lib/report_fmt.py` — `fmt_money`, `fmt_qty`, `qty_text`, `fmt_qty6`, `render_table`, `auto_aligns`, `align_columns`: the report's numbers and tables, below `src/taxjson/lib/out.py`, which renders with them.
- `src/taxjson/lib/account_report.py` — `build_account_report`: the account report JSON (work/<account>_report.json).
- `src/taxjson/lib/grant_writes.py` — `grant_write_expiries`, `grant_write_closes` (a grant-timing write that is the only record of its close: its expiry date and contracts, through the series' splits and renames).

## Output style and messages

All human output follows docs/output-style.md: wrapped prose, labelled
messages (Warning, Note, Error, ATTENTION), aligned tables. Stage messages are
captured during a run and re-worded for the console.

- `src/taxjson/lib/out.py` — `wrap`, `message`, `warn`, `note`, `attention`, `error`, `fail`, `fit_table`, `Doc`, `render`, `put`, `clean_json`, `JSON_SPEC`, `lint`, `console_lint`, `WIDTH`, `MAX_WIDTH`, `width`, `show`, `show_blocks`, `join_blocks`, `settle`, `settling_streams`: the house style; the report renderer (PLAN step 15: a `Doc` of ops, `exact` for a report whose prints it replaces, `render` its text or its `--json`, `JSON_SPEC` and `clean_json` the JSON's keywords and null for a non-finite float, `put` a plain line writer; `tests/features/output/test_report_renderer.py`); the style checks tests use; the wrap width (`TAXJSON_WIDTH`; 120 piped, the terminal's up to 160); a message printed with its lines flush-left and the one blank line a multi-line message owes its destination.
- `src/taxjson/lib/stage_msg.py` — `emit_line`, `say`, `reword`, `console_lines`, `is_continuation`: stage messages and the run console; `NOTE_HEAD`, `parse_codes_note`: the Questrade internal codes' note (`src/taxjson/lib/symbol_codes.py` — `codes_note` writes it).
- `src/taxjson/lib/out.py` — `act`, `act_lines`, `act_list`, `classify`, `prose_lines`, `details_hint`, `concise_on`, `concise_show`, `truncate`, `fold_summary`, `FOLD_SHOWN`, `CONCISE_ENV`: Essentials first — the `! ` lines to act on (`act`, `act_list`), the classification of a default view's lines (legend, table, act-on, prose) the style tests check (`classify`), the pointer to `--details`, a message cut to one line and the fold of a message said more than twice.
- `src/taxjson/lib/out.py` — `concise_line`, `detail_command`, `split_message`; `src/taxjson/lib/stage_msg.py` — `_CONCISE`, `env_concise`: a stage message as one console line (its headline and the command its detail names; the frequent wordy ATTENTION lines in a fixed short form).
- `src/taxjson/pipeline/messages.py` — `_details`; `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_brief`; `src/taxjson/cli/main.py` — `_brief_messages`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/run_state.py` — `current_details`; `src/taxjson/cli/parser.py` — `_DETAILS_CMDS`; `src/taxjson/cli/main.py` — `_OWN_MESSAGES`: every command's `--details`; the shared report warnings read `src/taxjson/pipeline/run_state.py` — `current_details`; `src/taxjson/cli/main.py` — `_brief_messages` folds the messages an engine prints while a command recomputes.
- `src/taxjson/lib/cli_diag.py` — `warn`, `error`, `note`, `read_text_utf8`, `write_text_atomic`, `InputReadError`: stderr diagnostics and safe reads and writes for tools.
- `src/taxjson/lib/trace_format.py` — `render_gain_block`, `render_report_trace`, `render_summary_table`, `render_document_header`: the per-gain trace boxes. `_report_window_lines`, `_render_wash_window`, `_settled_cells` (Canada's superficial-loss window, counted on settlement dates), `_render_wash_explanation` (its US part, IRC §1091: `_render_wash_us`), `_window_triggers` (each trigger of a denial), `_rules_line` (the rules a block's records name: the trace file's alone).
- `src/taxjson/lib/install_hint.py` — `extra_hint`, `INSTALLER`, `NOT_ON_PYPI`: the install lines the CLI prints.
- `src/taxjson/lib/json_contract.py` — `SCHEMAS`, `TOOL_OUTPUTS`, `COUNTRIES`, `versioned`: the `--json` contract (docs/settings.md, "`--json` outputs"): each output's `schema_version` in one registry (a command, a mode printing its own document, a tool's document no command prints), the lib modules' schema-version constants read from it, and `versioned`, which gives a doc its number and refuses another; `src/taxjson/pipeline/sanity.py` — `_json_out` takes the output's id (`schema=`). `tests/test_json_contract.py` — `PENDING_HOLD`, `UNHELD`, `output_of`, `_calls`: every `--json` command and tool has an entry, every `_json_out` and `versioned` call names its output, every golden `--json` view carries its number, every output is held by a view on its countries or a test that pins it (`UNHELD`), and settings.md lists each; `PENDING_HOLD` (plot-tax's US view, TJ-4921) only shrinks.
- `docs/output-style.md` — `Essentials first`, `Width`, `Messages`, `The run's console`, `Numbers, dates`: the style rules.

## Row listings

The listing commands read the native (before base-currency) per-account files
and print rows in .tt style, filtered by period, account, symbol and action.

- `src/taxjson/cli/listings.py` — `_run_tx_view` (its parts: `_tx_view_rows`, `_tx_view_text`), `cmd_transactions`, `cmd_dividends`, `cmd_dil`, `cmd_buysell`, `cmd_roc`, `cmd_gains`, `cmd_leaps`, `cmd_fees`, `cmd_transfers_view` (its parts: `_transfer_rows`, `_transfer_arrivals`, `_transfers_text`), `cmd_cash`; `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_view_window`, `_lone_positional` (a lone `[PERIOD] [ACCOUNT]` positional: an exact account name, `123abc`, is the account; one that is also a period, `2025`, the period with a warning), `_period_keep`, `_period_end`, `_tx_period_cutoff`, `_require_books`: `events`, `divs`; `src/taxjson/cli/listings.py` — dil, `trades`, `roc`, `gains`, `leaps`, `fees`, `transfers` (the TRANSFER evidence; a transfer-in is `own move` only when `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `transfer_arrivals`' `paired` names it; `src/taxjson/cli/listings.py` — `UNCLASSIFIED` when the classification fails) and `cash` (the cash events as `.tt` lines: `src/taxjson/lib/cash_events.py` — `tt_line`, `tt_comment`, `UNDECLARED`); the shared period filter (a look-back or to-date window ends today: `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_period_end`) and the "run first" check.

## Totals and positions

The `*-sum` commands total one row type per security or per year. `list` and
`shares` show positions from the canonical books (after ticker.map and
base-currency conversion, wash-adjusted where built). `technicals` lists the
same positions with statistics of their daily closes.

- `src/taxjson/cli/totals.py` — `cmd_divs_sum` (its text: `_divs_text`), `cmd_dil_sum`, `cmd_roc_sum` (`_roc_sum_other`, `_adjust_kind`: the ADJUST rows that are no return of capital), `cmd_trades_sum`, `cmd_fees_sum`, `cmd_ccd_sum` (its gains: `_ccd_gains`), `cmd_leaps_sum`, `cmd_winners`, `cmd_losers`, `_ranked_view` (winners and losers, one side each; its parts: `_ranked_gains`, `_ranked_text`); `src/taxjson/cli/positions.py` — `cmd_positions` (`_list_positional_date`: `list [ACCOUNT] [YYYY-MM-DD]`; `_positions_account_rows`: an account's rows; `_positions_table`: the text report; `_positions_as_of`, `_dated_inventory`: `list --date`, the cost timeline the account's canonical pass wrote; `src/taxjson/pipeline/freshness.py` — `timeline_beside`, a blended pool apportioned by `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_split_gains.py` — `_split_inventory`); `src/taxjson/cli/positions.py` — `cmd_shares`; `src/taxjson/cli/totals.py` — `_option_split_rows` (the option splits a grant-timing write follows, its split pool's); `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_box18_fractions`: totals by type; option totals and the ranked winners and losers; `list` and `shares`; `_window_gains_files`, `_gains_all_file`, `_gains_all_blend` (the accounts the run's cross-account pass blends, `src/taxjson/lib/report_model.py` — `wash_blend`, computed together), `_gains_artifact_scope`, `_warn_gains_artifact_scope`, `_leaps_scope_guard`, `_leaps_closed`: the gains files a windowed view (`stats`, `win-rates`, `winners`, `losers`, `ccd-sum`, `leaps`, `leaps-sum`) reads — the tax year's, or for a window outside it the full-history `work/<account>_gains_all.json` built on demand from `<account>_base.json`.
- `src/taxjson/cli/positions.py` — `held_by_symbol`: the books' end-of-data inventory summed per symbol, which `shares` and `technicals` read.
- `src/taxjson/cli/technicals.py` — `cmd_technicals` (the holdings and what is left out: `_holdings`; the benchmark, `--benchmark` else `[settings] benchmark`: `_benchmark`; the Yahoo spellings, a QUOTE line's else the price chain's: `_spellings`; the one-line refusal when no holding has a history: `_refuse_no_history`; the records: `_stats_rows`; `--json`: `_json_doc` (`_num`: no `-0.0`); the text: `_text`, its header `_header` (the benchmark's last close, marked when older than the rows'), its table `_main_table` under `LEGEND` (a last close older than the newest row's: `_older`, marked `*`); its `! ` lines: `_history_acts` (one line a cause: `_cause_act` for TAXJSON_OFFLINE, no [fx] extra and, when nothing came back at all, no answer, each naming the series a cache entry stood in for, offline past `OLD_CACHE_DAYS`; what the live tier did: `_Fetch`; the QUOTE advice, only when other symbols came back, and the benchmark's own line: `_quote_acts`), `_benchmark_acts` (BETA and ALPHA end on an older benchmark close; rows in another currency), `_act_named` (always the first name); its long form `_details_part`, `_notes`, each row's blanks and why `_blank_note`, `DEFINITIONS`), `register`: `tjs technicals`.
- `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `_PLANNING_SETTINGS`, `_VIEW_ONLY_SETTINGS`: `[settings] benchmark` is in both, so neither the run's digest of taxjson.toml (stale books, a filed year's frozen inputs) nor the checks' (kept checklist verdicts) sees it.
- `src/taxjson/lib/technicals.py` — `series_stats`, `blank_reasons` (why a statistic is blank: `TOO_SHORT`, `TOO_FEW_PAIRS`, `NO_BENCHMARK`, `FLAT`, `FLAT_BENCHMARK`, `NO_HIGH_LOW`, `NO_VOLUME`), `short_of`, `MIN_SESSIONS`, `Bar`, `sma_distance`, `rsi`, `beta_alpha`, `paired_returns`, `period_return`, `range_distances`, `volatility`, `max_drawdown`, `atr_pct`, `avg_volume`: the statistics, pure (no I/O): each one None with less history than its window.
- `src/taxjson/lib/price_chain.py` — `daily_history`, `DailyHistory` (its `cache_reason`: why a cached series was served, `HISTORY_TODAY` or why the live tier gave nothing), `_ask_history_tier`, `HistoryTierFailed` (a tier that raises: one warning line), `history_tier_installed`, `_yf_bar_rows`, `_history_bars`, `_cached_history`, `_history_hit`, `_history_entry`, `HISTORY_ADJUSTED`: `technicals`' daily sessions (Yahoo's split- and dividend-adjusted series through `_yf_history_fetcher`, a session dated today left out), cached in `work/.history_cache.json` with the day each was fetched.

## Summaries and estimates

`sum` prints the year's summary across accounts (gains, income, by form
line). `estimate` and `amt` estimate the tax and the Canadian minimum tax;
`instalments` schedules Canadian instalments; `plot-tax` charts the tax each
month's income generated against the estimate; `fx-cash` computes FX gains on
foreign cash; `stats` is a trader's win and loss view per asset class and
`win-rates` the same trades per ticker, account, month or class.

- `src/taxjson/cli/estimate.py` — `cmd_summary` (the text: a live exact Doc, `d`, each line printed as it is added, as the prints were, but its prints of a line not provably a str (a table's, `_out.wrap`'s, the act-on lines), which stay prints: the module redirects stdout, and a print of a value not provably a str skips str() when stdout is None; its parts write to it: `_sum_tables`, `_sum_return_us` and `_sum_return_ca` for FOR THE RETURN, `_sum_filing_positions`), `build_sum` (its figures and findings, the sum's refusals and stderr messages before any output: a `SumModel`; the estimate's warnings come with its text), `_sum_json` (the JSON), `_sum_inputs` (the inputs and the refusals), `_account_rows` (one row per account), `_account_aggregate`, `_return_figures` (FOR THE RETURN), `_fx_for_sum`, `_book_checks`, `_no_purchase_checks` (the checks: an act-on line each; a warning instead with --details, with `--json` or at width 0), `_report_aggregate_usable` (the report file's aggregate is served only when complete); `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_work_read_why` (a work/ file its reader refused, named once as `work/<file>` with the reason; exit 2); `src/taxjson/cli/estimate.py` — `_section_1256_gain`, `cmd_estimate`; `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `cmd_amt`, `_tax_estimate_result`; `src/taxjson/cli/estimate.py` — `_print_tax_estimate`, `_carry_back_or_warn` (the T1A carry-back the estimate prints and its `--json` carries), `_rounding_legend` (`sum`'s line on SUBTOTAL vs RETURN); `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `cmd_instalments`, `_instalment_config`, `cmd_fx_cash`, `cmd_stats`, `_closed_trade_inputs`, `_acb_pools` (a Canadian project's average-cost pools: the blended taxable accounts, each other account alone), `_acb_pool_timelines` (each pool's engine cost timeline: a spin-off's part of an assigned put's premium), `_fifo_books` (a US project's taxable accounts' books, shown or not: a move between your own accounts carries an assigned put's premium); `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_option_openings` (a missing-history option opening the option views add, as the gains stage does); `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `cmd_win_rates`, `_view_positionals` (the free-order SYMBOL / PERIOD / ACCOUNT positionals of `win-rates` and `option-yield`); `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_split_pool_of` (the accounts whose option splits are shared: Canada's blended taxable pool), `_gains_blended`: `sum`, `estimate`, `amt`, `instalments`, `fx-cash`, `stats` and `win-rates`.
- `src/taxjson/lib/readiness.py` — `build`, `conservation_lines`, `CONSERVATION_PREFIX`: whether a filing figure is complete, the readiness record of `sum --json` and `form-export --json` (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_form_export.py` — `--run-state-problem`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/tax_estimate.py` — `estimate_canada`, `estimate_usa`, `_canada_tax` (the foreign tax credit: T2209, then T2036), `_amt_canada` (its special foreign tax credit, s.127.54), `ca_amt_carryover`, `apply_vintage`, `CA_FED_BRACKETS`: brackets, credits and the AMT, by rate year; `section_1256_split` (US: Form 6781's 40% short-term / 60% long-term of the year's §1256 net, which the estimate, the carryover ledger and close-year's record use).
- `src/taxjson/lib/tax_plot.py` — `build`, `chart`, `render`, `slice_doc`, `months_of_year`, `HEIGHT`: `tjs plot-tax`, the monthly tax chart; `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `cmd_plot_tax` (the province, `_plot_tax_province`; the taxable accounts' gains read, `_plot_tax_docs`; the sales with an unknown cost counted, `_plot_tax_unknown_cost`), `_fold_estimate_income` (the estimator's inputs from one account's aggregates, shared with `sum` / `estimate`; each dividend Canadian or foreign by its own row's issuer country: `_issuer_facts`, `_issuer_split`, `_issuer_fact_at`), `_withholding_rows`, `_withholding_split`, `_withholding_total`, `_withholding_kwargs`, `_withholding_of_year`, `_actual_withholding` (the foreign tax withheld the estimate credits: the year's, or a month's through its end; the part the TAX rows hold, the part assumed at 15% and the spin-off deemed dividends nothing is assumed on), `_withholding_by_security` (the same withholding paired with each security's dividends, which `src/taxjson/lib/tax_estimate.py` — `ca_withholding_limit` caps at 15% of each security's own).
- `src/taxjson/lib/amt_report.py` — `build`, `render` (whether the minimum tax binds, `_render_binding`; a carryover recovered, `_render_recovery`), `render_recorded`: the AMT page.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_instalments.py` — `build`, `render`, `_render_rate`, `_render_interest_basis`, `render_brief`, `required_schedule`, `interest_and_penalty`; `src/taxjson/lib/instalment_rates.py` — `PUBLISHED_RATES`: instalment schedule, interest and penalty; the report's payments and rate line, and the basis interest is assessed on, are the renderer's two helpers.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_fx_cash.py` — `build_ledger`, `apply_jurisdiction`, `v2_verdict` (ledger v2's reportable figure: the $200 on currency gains only, debt gains in full), `render_report`, `render_brief`, `unreliable_status`, `CA_EXEMPTION`: FX gains on foreign-currency cash, the default ledger (v1: trades and income only, flagged NOT RELIABLE everywhere). The cash a row moves: `src/taxjson/lib/cash_legs.py` — `_flows`, `_cash_leg_dates` (a corporate action's cash paid after its row's date), `_broker_roc` (a broker's cash return of capital).
- `src/taxjson/lib/trade_stats.py` — `closed_trades`, `economic_pnl`, `compute`, `group`, `classify`, `written_option_trades` (an assignment's contract size: `contract_size`, the ticker.map MULT first), `share_credits` (an assigned option's premium drawn by the share rows its cost or proceeds went into: the accounts' lots replayed, first in, first out in a US project, a move between your own accounts carrying them; `_take_lots`; a US spin-off's part moved to the new shares' blocks: `_spin_part`; `credits_by_sale` reads one book alone), `pooled_put_credits` (a Canadian assigned put's premium drawn by its pool's sales, and by a transfer out of the pool: `_transfers_leaving`; the pool walked in the books' order: `_ca_book_order` and `_us_book_order` (the rows in each engine's order, `_engine_view`; the pairing twin `_assignment_legs` places the option rows among the legs with them, TJ-3219); a spin-off's part moved to the new shares, read from the engine's cost timeline: `_acb_drops`, `_acb_moved`), `_assignment_legs`, `CLASSES`, `GROUPINGS`: the closed trades, their statistics per class (`stats`) and per ticker, account, month or class (`win-rates`); a move in kind (a registered account's withdrawal in kind) is no trade, its P/L apart (`moved_in_kind`, backlog TJ-0629); `economic_pnl` is a gains row's P/L before any denial (its `raw_gain` plus the earlier denied loss its cost carries, the engines' `deferred_loss_recovered` in `src/taxjson/engine/ca/walk.py` and `src/taxjson/engine/us/trades.py`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/fx_cash_v2.py` — `build`, `_waits` (on one settlement day, an own move's arrival walked with its departure), `_promise`, `early_out` (an arrival the receiving broker books first: the units leave the sender then), `close_early`, `after_year`, `early_in` (the same across December 31: carried in the close), `_transit_spans` (own moves in transit inside the year, for `tjs t1135`), `close_transit` (an own move in transit at the year end, carried in the close), `_refund_legs` (a trade's folded commission refunds walked on their own dates), `_carry_openings`, `_carried_book`, `_book_account` (the prior year's close checked, never trusted, and each carried book placed by its broker account), `headline`, `render`, `TOL`, `LABEL`: the opt-in ledger v2 (debt per broker account, reconciliation to statement balances, refusals); `src/taxjson/lib/cash_lines.py` — `parse_line`, `opening_problem` (the `CASHOPEN` rule, which a carried book is held to too), `FORMS`, and the event builders `conv`, `move`, `balance`, `flow`; `src/taxjson/lib/cash_events.py` — `collect` (the cash events of the last run's parse, never the exports: each broker group's sidecar, `sidecar_path`, work/<account>_<broker>.cash, that `cash_sidecar` builds from the parse's events; a sidecar missing, unreadable or naming other files than the sources list refused naming `tjs run`, `_missing`, `_sidecar_files`; the parse writes it, `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_brokerage.py` — `--cash-out`, `_write_cash_out`; books older than the inputs refused first by `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_refuse_cash_older_than_inputs` over `_books_older_than_inputs`, then a sidecar missing or not the run's (its `CE.collect`), before any output, before the books' banner by `src/taxjson/cli/main.py` — `_banner_run_state`, `_reads_cash_events`, PLAN step 16 commit 6; every run sets the last full run's record aside before it writes into work/ and only a finished full run writes one again: `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_drop_run_records`, `_cmd_run`, `_run_folders`; `src/taxjson/lib/fingerprint_record.py` — `SET_ASIDE_FILE`; `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `record_input_fingerprint`, and run-clean's `_run_clean_inputs`, `_set_aside_changes`, `RUN_UNFINISHED`), `src/taxjson/lib/cash_events.py` — `read_project_lines`, `pair_internal`, `apply_declarations` (a .tt CASHMOVE line declaring a broker's move; the ledger and `tjs cash` share it), `Books`, `book_name`, `_short_width`: the cash-event book it reads (the `.tt` lines `FXCONV`, `CASHMOVE`, `CASHOPEN`, `CASHBAL`, `CASHBOOK` (`complete`), and each broker's events, its parse's (the entry below); `Books`, `unread`: the books of a broker whose parse builds none, which v2 refuses until declared complete); `src/taxjson/cli/estimate.py` — `_fx_cash_status`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_fx_cash_v2_doc`, `_fx_cash_ledger_choice`, `_fx_cash_carry`; `src/taxjson/cli/estimate.py` — `_fx_sum_item`: which ledger, the status `sum` / `checklist` / the run hook show, and the prior year's close (`fx_cash_v2` in the close-year record).
- `src/taxjson/lib/cash_events.py` — `cash_sidecar`: the cash events (the fx-cash ledger's events from each export), beside the fx-cash stage, written at the parse, each export's built by its broker's parse, for the brokers the registry marks, `src/taxjson/parsers/registry.py` — `builds_cash`, `cash_event_brokers` (lib's view: `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/base.py` — `CASH_EVENT_BROKERS`). They were read by a reader per broker in lib/cash_readers (step 5, TJ-2322), until PLAN step 16 commits 7 to 11 moved each broker's into its parse, one broker each, and commit 12 removed the folder: `tests/test_one_copy.py` — `test_the_cash_readers_are_gone`, `test_the_registry_names_the_parsers_that_build_cash_events`. RBC's since commit 7, `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/rbc_direct.py` — `rbc_cash_move`, `note_cash`, `cash_events` (classify notes each cash row, a copy of another download's in read, and the finish builds the file's events in its rows' order), Coinbase's since commit 8 (above: classify notes every row of a fiat currency or a cash coin and every Convert, the finish builds their events at the parse's stablecoin basis), Questrade's since commit 9 (above: classify notes every FXT, deposit, withdrawal, contribution, transfer-journal and LFJ row, the finish pairs the conversions' legs; taxjson-fetch asks the parse, `packages/taxjson-fetch/src/taxjson_fetch/api.py` — `_qt_cash_ledger_loses`), IB's since commit 10 (above: read notes the rows the reader read, as it read them, and the finish builds the events), Kraken's since commit 11 (above: classify notes every ledger row, and the finish builds the events with the account's other ledger exports read beside it, found by the parse's sibling finder (the tool's detection), `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/kraken.py` — `_finish_cash`, `_kr_cash_of`, `_kr_sibling_ledger_rows`, `_kraken_siblings`; a stablecoin note of another export's row is that export's parse's, backlog TJ-4461); a cash cell the parse itself never reads that the finish cannot read leaves the file no events and its problem, `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/base.py` — `_cash_events_from`, `cash_problem` (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_brokerage.py` — `_write_cash_out` hands it to `cash_sidecar` as the file's `problems`, worded as a reader's error was, never a refused parse); `src/taxjson/lib/cash_events.py` — `cash_sidecar` takes the parse's, `built`, and a file of a broker of `CASH_EVENT_BROKERS` without them is its problem, never an empty list read as no cash; the conformance kit reads the parse's, `src/taxjson/parsers/conformance.py` — `cash_events_of`. Tests get them from a parse with `tests/_support/parser_cash.py` — `parsed`, `parsed_cash_events` (a ValueError for the file's problem), `cash_sidecar`.

## Checks before filing

The checklist (`tjs checklist`) is every step from install to filing
in ten sections, each step proved by a command or marked from the
project's files; outside a project it prints the steps as a guide.
`--run` runs the checks (the slow ones `-j N` at a time, threads over
sub-commands: `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `_run_detectors`,
`default_jobs`, `SLOW`; the run's own readers three steps ask come in on
the Ctx, `RunHelpers`, built by `src/taxjson/cli/checklist_run.py` —
`checklist_run_helpers`) and keeps the verdicts in
`work/checklist_results.json` (`RESULTS_FILE`, `save_results`); without
`--run` they are shown as kept (`load_results`, `RunInfo`,
`run_info_lines`: when they were made, what moved since — each verdict
judged by its own basis (`_bases`, `_basis_now`, `_stale_reasons`: the
fingerprint of the run inputs and of the files and taxjson.toml
settings only the checks read, `checklist_fingerprint`,
`_check_only_files`, `_planning_config_digest`, and the lock `prior_year_record` names, resolved, or its absence, `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `_prior_record_entry`, `PRIOR_RECORD_KEY`: TJ-5089; the books' build time;
the version), the store read, merged and written under `_StateLock`,
another year's verdicts dropped and the shape's n/a never stored
(`_SHAPE_NA_DETAILS`); `NOT_CHECKED` for a
step never checked; `has_books`: a work/ holding only that file is no
work/ to the detectors). `sanity` compares the books against broker positions reports;
`check-dates` checks every trade time against market hours; `edge-cases`
lists rows whose treatment turns on a boundary.

- `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `d_missing_history`, `stored_unknown_cost`, `_unknown_cost_said`, `_unknown_cost_do`, `unknown_cost_keys`, `QUESTION_STEPS`, `question_answers`, `key_text` (the missing-history step: the find-missing-history rows to fix, and the year's rows at an unknown cost read from reports/run_summary.json, named with the cost to add, a question a `--done` mark answers per row — account, symbol, date, quantity, place, kind; backlog TJ-2770, TJ-3211, TJ-3213, TJ-3215, TJ-3216), `_layouts_read` (the layouts a folder's CSVs are read with, hashed by a frozen year and copied by the freeze), `_detail_cmd`, `_inputs_label`, `_do` (the closing line: for a finding the result's own `do`, else a blocked step's own remedy, else its `cmd`, else — a step with no standing advice — the command its detail names, as its `! ` line; the exports folder as the project sees it; a `! ` line's command is the result's `cmd`, else the one its detail names, else the step's first), `STEPS`, `US_STEPS`, `DETECTORS`, `evaluate`, `render` (the default view: the list — every step, an open step's commands, the next step's how and why; `LONG_FORM` and `--all` every step's — then `_summary_block`: the counts per section, one `!` line per step needing attention; `_title` names a `--section` / `--only` scope), `render_summary` (the summary alone, after a `--walk`), `resolve_id` (a step by its id or its number: `--done 18`), `resolve_section`, `section_ids`, `section_label` (`--section 4`, `--section fill`: one section shown, checked or walked), `once_progress`, `ACT_WIDTH`, `Ctx`, `Result`, `input_fingerprint`, `record_input_fingerprint`, `inputs_changed`, `LOCKED_YEARS_KEY` (a US project's filed-year locks, which its gains read: each year with its lock's content hash, `_locked_years_entry`, `_lock_paths`), `load_state`, `set_override`, `_SAVED_ID_MIGRATION` (a mark saved in checklist.json under the tips item's former id is read as the tips item's), `d_journals`, `d_renames`: the checks, one detector per check; the git checks (`d_inputs_committed`, `d_lock_committed`) go through `_git` (hooks and fsmonitor off) and `_git_status`, and are blocked by `_git_refusal` when the repository's own config names a command (`_GIT_COMMAND_KEYS`: a filter, a diff textconv); whether inputs changed since the last run, and saved marks. The list itself: `SECTIONS`, `items` (each `Item`'s section, title, `Cmd` commands, how and why; `_spec` holds them in order), `item_ids`, `item_meta`, `STEP_RULES` (the steps no detector proves: `s_configure`, `s_transfers`, `s_ticker_map`, `s_format` (the map the run reads, ticker.map with its tobase.map: `_map_problems` names the first line the run refuses and marks the loader's lines said, `mark_map_problems_said` in `src/taxjson/lib/ticker_map.py`), ... — from the project's files and `reports/run_summary.json`, `src/taxjson/lib/first_run.py` — `collect`, `SUMMARY_FILE`), `_before_run`, `next_result`, `_project_cmds` (each item as it applies in the project, in the list and `--json`: configure's `tjs migrate` / `tjs align --from` only where they work, explore's `tjs staking` only with a crypto account, `_crypto_configured`; a filed year's inputs-frozen step, `FROZEN_INPUTS_HOW`), `render_guide`, `to_json`, `guide_json`, `SCHEMA_VERSION`, `named_commands`, `EXCLUDED` (every user-facing command named by an item or excluded with its reason; `tests/test_checklist_items.py` checks both ways).
- `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `d_inputs_frozen` (`_inputs_reach`; done once the year is filed, `_filed_after_year_end`, `_year_filed`: a late export is an amendment), `d_lock_committed` (`_filed_ignored`: the lock or the reports as filed hidden by `.gitignore`, named with its rule), `s_next_year` (a single-folder project: `migrate --to-years` first): the year-end steps.
- `src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `cmd_checklist` (a DONE mark on a checked step prints that a finding outranks it; a `--done` over a finding this year's kept verdict holds is refused before any mark is written, and `--force` records the forced mark, `_checklist_marks`, the walk's `[d]` and `[f]` alike: `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `kept_verdict`, `finding_over_done`, decided by the verdict's own `question` flag, not the step); `src/taxjson/cli/checklist_run.py` — `_checklist_sections` (the old `--skip` refused and `--section` read before the project is looked for), `_checklist_results` (the verdicts checked and kept with `--run`, else the kept ones), `_checklist_config` (taxjson.toml read leniently when the strict loader refuses it: the configure item's finding, and every check blocked; an account that is no table, or a folder link leaving the project, is refused as every command refuses it; `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `_refuse_outside_dir_links`; `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `Ctx`, `CONFIG_FIRST`), `_checklist_walk` (exit 1 while a step is open, the marks applied), `_die_state` (a checklist.json that cannot be read or written); `src/taxjson/pipeline/sanity.py` — `cmd_sanity` (in parts: `_sanity_books`, `_sanity_nothing_to_compare`, `_sanity_country`, `_sanity_costs`; the text: a live exact Doc, `d`, each line printed as it is added, as the prints were, which cmd_sanity passes to its parts: `_sanity_groups_text`, `_sanity_unchecked_text`, `_sanity_verdict_text`), `_read_books` (each account's positions from its gains file, and the accounts whose file cannot be read), `_renames` (the ticker.map renames the files' symbols take), `_sanity_json_doc`, `_nothing_to_compare_json` (the `--json` documents), `_Placement` (the items placed in their groups), `_Files` (each holdings file read once: `_ext_book`, `_rows_of`), `_config_groups`, `_no_groups_refusal` (taxjson.toml pairs nothing: the notes, or the refusal), `_group_items` (the groups compared: taxjson.toml's pairs, then the items), `_compare_unit` (one unit of a group compared with the books), `_ext_costs`, `_cost_rows`, `_cost_notes` (the cost compare), `_no_books` (an account of taxjson.toml with no books: no inputs, naming its folder, or a run that did not build them), `_project_file` (a relative FILE of `sanity` / `opening` looked for in the project folder when the shell's has none), `_sanity_items_from_config`, `_sanity_print_extras`; `src/taxjson/cli/explain.py` — `cmd_check_dates`, `cmd_edge_cases`, `cmd_spinoffs`, `cmd_splits`: `checklist` (and its interactive walk), `sanity`, `check-dates`, `edge-cases`, `spinoffs` and `splits`.
- `src/taxjson/lib/positions_reports.py` — `read_positions`, `detect_positions`, `PositionsReport`, `PositionRow`, `positions_only`, `toml_as_of`, `PositionsDateError` (an impossible date: sanity refuses the file), `PositionsSnapshotError` (not a whole snapshot: a `holdings_count` that is not its number of tables, a contract size that is not a positive number; sanity refuses the file), `account_key` (the whole broker account id hashed, for telling accounts apart while labels stay masked): broker positions reports (IB, RBC, TOML holdings).
- `src/taxjson/lib/positions_reports.py` — `_RBC_PER_SHARE_RE`, `_RBC_AVERAGE_RE`, `_rbc_cost_label_error`: an RBC cost column read as a total or per share by its label, refused when it is neither.
- `src/taxjson/lib/positions_check.py` — `compare_cost` (`roc_symbols`, `spinoff_symbols`: its reasons), `positions_on`, `book_rows`, `load_base_rows`, `BaseBookError` (`strict`: books that are there but unreadable are refused, never an empty history), `income_share_mismatches` (entitlement on the record date, else `INCOME_WINDOW_DAYS` before the pay date), `load_inventory`: cost and dated-position checks for `sanity` (`book_rows`: the books' rows with the missing-history openings the gains run booked — every dated positions view reads them; `src/taxjson/pipeline/sanity.py` — `cmd_sanity`, `_split_by_date`, `_file_day`, `_file_broker`: several snapshots of one broker account compared one date at a time; files of one account at different dates that do not name their broker account refused; `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `d_sanity`, `_sanity_differences`: the checklist's step, `tjs sanity`'s own verdict (the run compares nothing), named by the quantity differences, never the informational COST section).
- `src/taxjson/pipeline/sanity.py` — `_cash_coin`, `_books_hold`, `_skip_cash_coin`, `note_cash_coins`: a crypto snapshot row of a USD stablecoin, in a project whose books hold stablecoins as US-dollar cash (`src/taxjson/rules/country.py` — `stablecoin_model`; the parsers' own classification, `src/taxjson/lib/markets.py` — `is_usd_stablecoin`, the code read as the Kraken parser reads an asset code, wallet suffixes included: `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/kraken.py` — `_normalize_asset`) and carry no position of that coin in the accounts compared (a non-zero inventory quantity, or a row that buys, sells or moves it as property; a reward's income row is none), is left out of the quantity and cost compares as a cash row is, with a note naming the coin (the `--json` `notes` too); a position of it (a `.tt` BUYSELL's) is compared as any coin, and a US project compares the row as a coin (backlog TJ-4950). `_ext_book`, `_OPT_RE`: a holdings file's option keyed by the exchange's option root, matched to the books' contract through its `underlying` (`matched_via_underlying`), an option on a future in each prefix spelling (`src/taxjson/lib/futures_prefixes.py` — `FUTURES_PREFIX_GROUP`, backlog TJ-5037).
- `src/taxjson/lib/export_coverage.py` — `find_gaps`, `account_gaps`, `_units` / `_unit_gap` / `_merge` (each broker account of a combined account against its own statements), `open_positions`, `_moment` (a split leads its day in a Canadian project), `held_at_broker` (a broker's position counts as far as the account's books hold it), `closed_later` (the account's later rows of any source, `.tt` included, close a position; `spent`: a row's units close once across the account's brokers; `_spend_own_units`: a broker's rows close its own units first), `next_activity` / `list_command` (the checklist's line: a count, `tjs list <account> <end>` and the next activity a later file records), `file_source_id` / `row_source_ids` / `add_source` / `source_broker` (a book row's export, by the masked name the parse writes), `_book_renames` (sidecar and corporate-action rows through the books' renames), `info_message`, `ib_statement_end`, `webull_export_end`, `message`, `question_text`, `detail`, `Gap` (`key`: account|broker|end; `expired`: options that expired in the gap), `GRACE_DAYS`, `LAST_ROW_SLACK`: a broker whose exports for an account end before the year end (today in the running year) while it holds positions there; the run's Warning (`src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_say_export_coverage`, `_checklist_answered`, `_mark_answers`), the checklist's export-coverage step (`src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `d_export_coverage`; a `Result` with `question` set is answered by a DONE mark for the `answers` keys it recorded: `question_answers`, `apply_override`, `QUESTION_STEPS`, `option_keys`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/check_dates.py` — `analyze`, `render`, `check_trade_time`, `asset_class`, `sources`: `tjs check-dates` (a parsed source the account's sources list names is checked, or an ERROR: `unreadable`, `missing`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/edge_cases.py` — `analyze`, `render_text` (the year-boundary sections, `_render_year_boundary`; how --details counts the window, `_render_how_counted`), `year_straddles`, `windows_across_year_end`, `_allowed_why`, `_us_allowed_why`, `deferred_into_next_year`, `calls_in_windows`: `tjs edge-cases` (the engine's year-end pools or lots, `inventory_year_end`: `src/taxjson/engine/ca/holders.py` — `final_year_end_pools`, `year_end_basis`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/taxrules.py` — `_ye_lots`; `src/taxjson/engine/us/prepare.py` — `_us_inventory_rows`: Canada's pools and the US lots, a US settle project's on settlement dates).

## Audit, wash sales, explain and tax logic

`audit` traces every taxable disposition from the broker row to the reported
gain. `wash-sales` lists superficial losses or wash sales with the replacement
that caused each. `taxjson-explain` prints a calculation trace for one gain.
`tax-logic` prints every rule the code applies, with the project's settings
filled in; it is the spec the code and tests are checked against.

- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_audit.py` — `main`, `_audit_books`, `_audit_engine`, `_audit_manual_tie`, `_audit_reconcile`, `_audit_output`, `build_event`, `render_event`, `_render_event_origin`, `_render_event_wash`, `render_reconciliation`, `build_source_index`, `merge_lot_records`: the audit (`main` reads the books and their indexes, re-runs the engine, ties the manual-reporting rows, reconciles and prints, each in its helper, in that order; `render_event` prints one disposition; its SOURCE, MAPPING and CURRENCY lines and its gain and WASH section are its two helpers).
