What it compares: `tie_out_differences`, `manual_differences`, `TIE_FIELDS` (the saved gains file, field by field and per lot; a row's figures read through model/result `GainRow` views, `_figure`, `_sum`; `gain_total`, the computed gain, a manual-reporting sale left out, and `disallowed_total`, which `tjs audit --json` over several books sums the same way), `money_lineage` (a book row's amount from its parsed row, converted or not; a settled futures fill's from `futures_native_nets`, the parsed rows of its account and symbol settled as the run settles them, each broker cancellation netted first as the merge nets it (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_merge2.py` — `cancel_trade_pairs`), and one it cannot place named on the symbol's settled rows, `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_audit.py` — `FuturesNets`, `_unplaced`: TJ-5193; `row_money_field`: a dividend's gross; `_moneyless_split`: a split with no money needs no rate), `pool_trade_check` (the audited securities' buys and earlier trades), `pool_other_rows_check` (their rows that are not trades), `pool_scope` (the securities read: under earlier tickers, across a corporate action; `_row_day`: a row's day, a record date counting), `source_hits`, `_map_note` (the MAPPING line: ticker.map, or the run's effective map, `--effective-map`), `build_source_index` (a row with no id in its file — a corporate action's — under the id the merge gives it); a row with no parsed row, `row_origin` (what made it: a `[[distributions]]` table, a `.tt` RENAME line from `load_dated_renames`, missing history, a stage of the run) and `untraced_rows` (the `--details` list); what it does not, `SCOPE` (the `--json` `scope`, the footer's `not traced` line). A `.tt` row's line: `src/taxjson/lib/tt_format.py` — `tt_to_json`, `source_lines`; a corporate action's statements: `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_corp_actions.py` — `statements`.
- `src/taxjson/cli/explain.py` — `cmd_audit` (its parts: `_audit_flags`, `_audit_invocations`, `_audit_report`), `_merge_audit_json`, `_stopped_at` (the accounts left unaudited when a book's audit stops at an error: `--json` `not_audited`), `cmd_wash_sales` (its parts: `_wash_sales_rows`, `_wash_sales_embedded`, `_wash_sales_table`), `_explain_wash_sales`, `cmd_tax_logic`: `audit`, `wash-sales` and `tax-logic`. The parsed-source files the audit joins: `src/taxjson/lib/project_layout.py` — `_audit_source_files`, `_AUDIT_DERIVED_SUFFIXES` (the work/ files that are derived books, never provenance).
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_explain.py` — `main`, `print_trace`, `gain_matches` (`--account`: one account's dispositions of a merged book), `fmt_summary`: the `taxjson-explain` tool.
- `src/taxjson/lib/tax_logic.py` — `Rule`, `catalog`, `sections`, `render`, `_canada`, `_usa`, `PARTITION_RULES`, `ENGINE_RULES`, `NON_RULE_SETTINGS`, `_sentences`, `_clauses`: the rule statements with their ids; `render` prints a statement's first sentence as its bullet and the rest as items under it; `ENGINE_RULES`, the rules an engine or characterisation record names (PLAN step 11).
- `src/taxjson/rules/ids.py` — `ALL`, `CANADA`, `USA`, `CA_EST_PROVINCE_TABLES`, `CTRY`, `CA_SL_02`: every rule id as a constant (step 7), imported as `R`: a leaf every layer and lib may import (`scripts/check_architecture.py` `LEAVES`); the statements in tax_logic name their ids by it, and code that applies a rule may.

## Planning before you trade

These views answer "what happens if I trade now": the wash radar shows each
holding's superficial-loss or wash-sale status as of a date; buy-check and
sell-check answer for one symbol; rebuy lists each loss sale whose window is
open with the day it closes; div-estimate projects the coming dividends; harvest shows unrealized gains at current
prices; tips gives tax-efficiency advice for next year (for example a
Canadian dividend payer held through its US listing); watch reports changes
since the last look (cron-able); option-boundary lists written options that
straddle a year end.

- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_wash_radar.py` — `main` (the arguments, the books, `_Radar`'s steps, then `_radar_rows` and `_output`), `_parser`, `_load_books` (every book's rows, in the engine's input order; a book in another currency than the return's refused: `_other_currency`), `_prepare_books` (transfers netted, settle-lag splits, the missing-history openings), `_radar_rows` (the per-ticker loop: the rows shown), `_output` (the report printed, the --json document written or printed), `_payload` (the --json document, from the report), `_render_text`, `_render_brief` (the default STATUS table), `_us_engine_losses`, `_EngineLosses` (the --gains files' losses), `_advisory_category`, `_CA_DEFINITIONS`: the wash radar. `_Radar`: its books and the state its methods read; `load`, then `walk` (each booked row moves its account's pool: `_walk_adjust`, `_walk_split`, and `_walk_trade`, which records the acquisitions and losses), then `decide` (the US engine's verdicts, or Canada's claims and then the empty `_today_ledger` the forward views read), `scope` for --show. `_Pos`: one ticker's position for its verdict (its class, the units held, the losses in their windows and the VIOLATIONs, each holder's units and calls; `_held_by_holder`, `_backing_us`, `_full_position_span`); `_Verdict`: what the per-ticker branches set (the advisory, its clear date, the JSON record's fields). The verdicts of a ticker with losses in their windows, one function per branch of `_radar_rows`'s loop: `_v_us_exempt` (a US §1256 contract), `_v_claimed` (ALLOWLOSS), `_v_washed` (US), `_v_violation` with `_rescue` (Canada), `_v_blocked` (BLOCKED, COOLING, a US short cover); then `_v_long_call_note` (US: a long call bought in a loss's window). Each ticker's view after them, in the loop's order: `_v_forward` (a loss sale today: LOCKED, EXITABLE or CAUTION; its backing from `_v_forward_backing`, on the today ledger in Canada), `_v_risk`, `_v_passed_and_short_cover` (a VIOLATION past its rescue date, then a US short cover's COOLING or note), `_v_clear`. Then the row: `_row_notes` (its warn-only notes: each added to the advisory once, and returned one line each for the tools that print their own verdict line), `_loss_records` (the class's losses for `tjs rebuy`, and `rebuy_at`), `_recent_buy` (the class's latest purchase in a loss sale today's window, in Canada one its buyer still holds: the account and date sell-check's SIMILAR caveat names), `_row_record` (the row: a `_RadarRow` of its JSON record and `claimed_only`, which is in the text table only). `RadarReport`: the rows sorted once (by category, then ticker; the JSON sections read the same order), `by_cat` (each category's rows as their `cells`, the six cells `_render_text`, `_render_brief` and `_claim_state` read), the sections, the SIMILAR groups and lines; `render`. `_capacity`, `_claim`: each Canadian loss's per-holder backing; `_acq_within`: a class's acquisitions in a window, by bisect, TJ-0239. `_sale_epoch` (a loss sale today's window date: its settlement in Canada), `_safe_sale_date` (the date a LOCKED, EXITABLE or CAUTION row clears, as `reentry_date` gives a buy's). `--ticker-map`, `_listings_booked_under`, `_calendars`: a listing ticker.map / tobase.map books as another is dated on both exchanges' calendars (`src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_radar_engine_args`). `_per_unit_denial`, `_buy_replaces`: buy-check's per-unit share of the in-window losses (`recent_loss_per_unit`). `--show`, `_shown_for_line`, `rows_scoped`: `wash-radar ACCOUNT` and the run's `reports/wash_radar_<account>.*` read every taxable book and show one account's listings (`src/taxjson/cli/plan.py` — `cmd_wash_radar`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `stage_cross_reports`).
- `src/taxjson/cli/plan.py` — `cmd_wash_radar`; `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_radar_config`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_radar_engine_args`; `src/taxjson/cli/plan.py` — `cmd_buy_check` (one symbol's verdict across its class, `_buy_check_class_verdict`, and each radar row's part of it by category, `_buy_check_row_verdict`), `cmd_sell_check` (each radar row's part of its verdict by category, `_sell_check_row_verdict`), `cmd_rebuy`, `_wash_class_context`, `cmd_harvest` (the wash-radar reports it reads, a live radar for stale ones, `_harvest_radar_files`), `cmd_watch` (its state file, `_watch_state_path`; this run's harvest total, `_watch_harvest_total`, and its comparison with the baseline's, `_watch_harvest_compare`); `src/taxjson/cli/tips.py` — `cmd_tips` (its per-listing positions, `_tips_holdings`; the dividend payers, `_tips_dividends`; the ticker.map tables, sightings and the six checks they answer, `_Listings`; the offline checks, US-LISTING and TFSA-US-DIV, `_offline_tips`; the --online probe, `_online_tips`, and its issuer names, `_name_cluster_tips`); `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `cmd_option_boundary` (its parts: `_option_boundary_books`, `_option_boundary_text`): wash-radar, buy-check, sell-check, rebuy, harvest, watch, tips and option-boundary. `src/taxjson/cli/plan.py` — `_radar_taxable_bases` (the taxable books a radar run reads; `_radar_missing_context`, the ones missing, after which `watch` compares and saves nothing: `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_watch.py` — `baseline_date`); `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_not_applicable` / `_no_wash_checkable` (no account the rule reaches: one line, or the command's `--json` document with `not_applicable`; `src/taxjson/cli/plan.py` — `_empty_radar_json`, `_check_na_json`). `src/taxjson/cli/plan.py` — `_with_forward_rows`, `_window_over`, `_passed_violation_text`: a VIOLATION whose rescue date has passed, beside the radar row's `forward` view of a loss sale today (sell-check, buy-check).
- `src/taxjson/lib/rebuy.py` — `build`, `render`, `any_account_line`, `CLEARED_DAYS`: `tjs rebuy`, each loss sale whose window is open and its re-entry date, read from the radar's rows (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_wash_radar.py` — `reentry_date`, `rebuy_at`, `--cleared-days`, `_loss_records`; `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_watch.py` — `flatten_radar`), matched to a query as buy-check matches (`src/taxjson/cli/plan.py` — `_class_matches`, `canon.members`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/div_estimate.py` — `payments` (one payment per account, broker account, symbol, date and currency; `splits` scale earlier share counts, every split in the books whatever the as-of date, one event per `split_copies`; `_now` gives a payment's symbol now, through renames, and scales it by the splits after its record date; `qty_by` adds a dividend's shares to a payment in lieu's, `_short_on` tells a short's expense from a reversal by the position `_held` walks: each account's trades, split-adjusted, through renames, and each broker account's own, `held_src`; a reversal's TAX refund moves with the gross it cancels; `tax_fx` keeps a withholding posted in another currency, converted by `estimate` at the pay date's rate, `_rate_on`), `Payments` (re-read as of the estimate's date; `notes` for the flags; `short_paid`, the dividends paid while the books show a short, for the command's warning), `schedule`, `detect_schedule` (the frequency grid and the latest payment on it; `detect_frequency` without it), `assumed_quarterly` (a history too short to settle a schedule: quarterly, the row's `frequency_assumed`, until the books contradict it; `_date_reading`: what the books say of a date of the grid with no payment (quarterly, or a settled payer's own, `_period`), from `_held_now`, the balances per security named now (`_held`'s lists cut at each rename: an old symbol taken up again after it is another security's; each broker account's own, so one's short never nets away another's long, TJ-5051, unless a sale with no broker short marker puts one short, `src/taxjson/lib/missing_history.py` — `broker_short_marker`), and `_paid_and_ends`, every income row's date and where each account's books end; `_long_through`, a move between accounts under way on the date held), `_on_schedule` (the payment the forecast runs from), `_schedule_read` (the schedule as `estimate` reads it: assumed, or settled with the dates of its grid on which the books show the shares not held excused and named, `_not_held`, where the walk ends at the shares held now and shows shares held before each payment, `_walk_agrees`: TJ-5050; `estimate`'s `held`, every account's shares held now, whatever account is asked for), `estimate`, `horizon_problem`, `render` (`_how`: its HOW IT IS ESTIMATED lines), `FREQUENCIES`, `GRACE_DAYS`: `tjs div-estimate`, upcoming dividends from each held position's own history (`LOOKBACK` payments on a frequency's grid); `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `cmd_div_estimate` (the native books, the native holdings, every account's `held` and the asked account's `positions`, `latest_rate`, `horizon_problem`); `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_tt_openings` (the units `.tt` `OPENING … cost=unknown` lines open, as the gains stage opens them).
- `src/taxjson/lib/option_yield.py` — `contracts`, `analyze`, `render` (each currency's realized P/L and yield, `_render_totals`; the `! ` lines, `_render_acts`), `Lot`, `Close`, `underlying_matches`, `default_size`, `units_on`, `symbol_on` (a lot's dated splits), `_finish` (the capital, an unknown premium): `tjs option-yield`, an underlying's option trades over a window (contracts matched per account and series FIFO; premiums, realized P/L by how each closed, open contracts, the yield on the cost of the shares held, else the time-weighted strike notional, annualized); `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `cmd_option_yield` (the native books of every account, the positionals resolved as underlyings / period / account, the window's start and end; a split's place in its day as the country's engine orders it, `split_at_open`; the project's ticker.map; `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `_use_project_ticker_map`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/option_yield.py` — `strike_on`, `notional_on`, `size_on`, `_series_size`: a renamed series' own strike and contract size; `_add_notional_days` (the strike notional of each stretch between a lot's splits and renames), `seq` (the opening's replay order: FIFO when a rename merges two series), `TRANSFERRED`, `transfer_in`, `_kept_transfer_ids` (a TRANSFER row moves contracts out — no money, no P/L — or in, premium unknown; a zero-net cluster is a no-op, as the gains run reads it).

- `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `_attention_reason` (the checklist's option-boundary line: the first flagged contract's reason); `src/taxjson/lib/option_boundary.py` — `write_lots` (per account and symbol; `factor`, `seq`, `origin` on each `WriteLot`: the splits since the write, the write's replay order, whether the write is in the books), `kept_transfers` (the TRANSFER rows the gains run keeps: a zero-net cluster is a no-op), `TRANSFERRED` (a close that moved the contracts out), `_unknown_rows` (a short opening balance or a transfer in: premium and write year unknown, said so), `straddling`, `expired_open`, `filed_locks`, `WriteLot`, `project_question_rows`, `question_message`, `question_detail`: written options across a year boundary, and the transition question (a contract written before `option_grant_timing_since` closed this year, CA-OPT-11) that `tjs run` (`src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_say_option_transition`), and the checklist (`src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `d_option_boundary`) ask.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_lint_crosslistings.py` — `analyze`, `venue_splits`, `main`: cross-listed holdings the radar would see as two.
- `src/taxjson/lib/price_chain.py` — `fetch_prices`, `fetch_option_prices`, `PriceQuote`, `yf_symbol_for`, `latest_rate`: current prices (IBKR, then yfinance, then the cache).
- `src/taxjson/lib/price_chain.py` — `close_on`, `DayClose`, `OfflineCloseMissing`: one day's close (the close cache, then Yahoo's history) for an in-kind move's value.
- `src/taxjson/lib/price_chain.py` — `provider_unit`, `clean_quote_currency`, `minor_unit_listing`: the one minor-unit table (GBp/GBX, ZAc, ILA -> major) the live quote, the day's close and both caches share.
- `src/taxjson/lib/price_chain.py` — `_money`, `_usable_price`, `_ibkr_cache_spelling`, `_ibkr_currency`: the one price-value rule (a finite number above 0, never a bool) for the tiers and the caches; an IBKR hit is cached under the listing it priced and carries that listing's currency.
- `src/taxjson/lib/price_chain.py` — `_quiet_yfinance`, `_yfinance_fetcher`, `_yf_history_fetcher`: the Yahoo tiers, with yfinance's own log and print lines silenced (the callers' labelled warnings say what was missed).
- `src/taxjson/lib/price_chain.py` — `_load_cache`, `_cached_price`, `_save_cache`, `_iso_day_in`: the two caches' read, and the save of a run's fresh entries merged into the file under its lock (`src/taxjson/lib/json_cache.py` — `save_json_cache`); a cached close's day must lie in the asked day's lookback window, a cached quote's date may not be after today (`CACHE_CLOCK_SKEW_DAYS`), and an entry whose unit cannot be read is a miss.

## Filing forms and slips

`form-export` renders gains into filing shapes (Schedule 3 lines for Canada,
Form 8949 and TXF for the US). `t1135` helps with the foreign property form.
`reconcile-slips` compares the broker's T5008 or 1099-B slips with the
computed dispositions; `slip-audit` (Canada) compares the T5 and T3 slips
with the books' income. `carryover` keeps the loss carry-forward ledger.

- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_form_export.py` — `main`, `_main`, `_export_manual`, `_export_txf`, `_export_report`, `build_txf`, `filing_lines`: the form renderers (`_main` builds the form; its unknown-cost rows, `--form txf` and the printed or `--json` report are its three helpers); `write_csv` (`--csv`) writes each cell through `spreadsheet_cell` (a formula-looking text cell gets a leading quote). The rows they render: `src/taxjson/lib/tax_forms.py` — `build_schedule3`, `schedule3_line`, `build_8949`.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_t1135.py` — `build_report`, `render_report`, `_render_property_tables`, `_render_notes`, `render_brief`, `walk_costs`, `classify_country`, `FILING_THRESHOLD`, `main`, `_parse_args`: T1135 cost amounts by country (the tool's command line read in `_parse_args`; the report's per-property and per-country tables, and its notes, in the renderer's two helpers).
- `src/taxjson/lib/t1135_issuer.py` — `Countries`, `decide`, `with_lines`, `overrides`, `_chain_line`, `_security`, `_other_holdings`, `EARLIER`, `EARLIER_COUNTRY`, `Holdings`, `_pool_days`, `_long`, `securities`, `in_year`, `shared`, `Leg`, `Evidence`, `Decision`, `facts`, `_for_year`, `prefix_country`, `says_receipt`, `is_share`, `TRANSFER_BRIDGE_DAYS`: the country of each security for the T1135 and what decided it (tax-logic CA-RPT-02, CA-RPT-21; TJ-4957): a ticker.map T1135 line, the ISIN an export gives and tobase.map's issuer country, the same security's ISIN in another account (learned within the run, nothing saved), else the listing (`assumed`); the books' rows walked into legs (one unbroken holding of a symbol over every account) and securities (legs a ticker change joins: a rename of one security, never a merger or a ticker bought again), so a T1135 line decides a renamed security before and after its change and nothing crosses a reused ticker; sources of one rank that disagree, and an ISIN prefix no country has, are REVIEW, never a pick; `src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/ib_extractor.py` — `_ib_stamp_security_ids`, `_ISIN_RE`, `_IB_RECEIPT_TYPES`: each Interactive Brokers share row's `isin` (and `security_type` for a depositary receipt) from the statement's Financial Instrument Information section; `src/taxjson/model/transaction.py` — `isin`, `isins`, `security_type`: the row fields (`isin` exactly one ISIN; `isins` the two a statement gives one ticker on one market).
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_t1135.py` — `classification_check`, `verdict_keys`, `UNRESOLVED`, `VERDICT_HOLDS`, `_issuer_conflicts`, `_isin_conflict`, `_receipt_conflict`, `_earlier_conflict`, `_mismatch_conflicts`, `_line_symbol`, `_unused_overrides`, `_isin_words`, `foreign_isin_symbols`, `issuer_mismatch_symbols`, `_by_listing`, `_row_symbols`, `_row_source`, `_country_report`, `_country_review`, `_review_words`, `_walk_as`, `_walk_with`, `_custody_and_cash`, `_verdict_open`, `_verdict_head`, `_verdict_tail`, `_part`, `_brief_verdict`, `_line`, `_acb_unknown`, `_cash_open`, `_conflict_words`, `_from`, `_from_notes`, `_country_acts`, `_assumed_act`, `_said_review`, `_country_warns`, `_render_country_lists`, `_overruled`: the report's side of the country (the FROM column and `country_source` of each row, `country_decisions`, the securities an ISIN counts otherwise than their listing, the assumed ones with their cost, the ones to review), and the test while a holding's being foreign property is open: a share whose sources disagree (counted as foreign under `??`) and a listed option whose listing and share disagree (CA-RPT-22; TJ-4963), walked again with the lines the text names (`--json` `classification_status`, `filing_required` true / false / null; TJ-4956, TJ-4957); `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `_t1135_unresolved`, `_t1135_review`, `_t1135_assumed`: the checklist step then (attention while the test is open or a security is to review; the assumed ones counted in its detail).
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_t1135.py` — `full_history_pass`, `_engine_timeline`, `walk_costs`, `_Pool`, `eps`, `cost_amount`, `held_in_year`, `unknown_in_year`, `long_in_year`, `short_in_year`, `short_only_symbols`, `Custody`, `units`, `units_at`, `change_dates`, `_custodian_arg`: the cost amounts are the gains engine's own pools — one full-history engine pass (the taxable books together, registered accounts as wash context, the project's missing-history file and option timing) and its cost timeline, read day by day (backlog TJ-0417: no replay of the books); a pool's flat tolerance is the engine's (`pool_qty_eps`: a sub-micro coin holding is property); a holding at a known zero cost stays a row, a cost unknown while held stays unknown for the year, and a position held only short (a written option, a short sale) is named instead of a row; a Canadian issuer's shares held with a custodian outside Canada (`[accounts.NAME] custodian_country`, validated in `src/taxjson/lib/config_check.py` — `account_type_problems`, read by `src/taxjson/lib/t1135_country.py` — `parse_custodian_country`, passed by `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `cmd_t1135`) are a pool of their own (SYMBOL@ACCOUNT) at the account's share of the engine's pool, its units at each engine step (`units_at`: the day's end less its rows the engine walks later that day, backlog TJ-0646; from its books, as `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_split_gains.py` — `_split_inventory` apportions one) times the pool's cost per unit, the rest of the pool under the symbol: a row of category 6 under the custodian's country (backlog TJ-0369, TJ-0417); `gains_not_given`, `_gains_accounts`, `_amt`: what the standalone tool reads without its gains file has n/a income and gain, not 0 (backlog TJ-0355).
- `src/taxjson/lib/t1135_cash.py` — `_base_spans` (the base currency's own moves in transit, named, not counted; TJ-1146); `counted_accounts`, `cash_doc`, `merge`, `check`, `_transit` (cash in transit between your own accounts at the year end, one entry per departure and arrival: under one country with both accounts counted, else named and unknown; TJ-0946, TJ-1186, TJ-1187; on trade dates, also what trades in the year: TJ-1157, TJ-1185); `src/taxjson/lib/fx_cash_v2.py` — `_base_transit`, `_base_holdings`, `record_op`, `holdings`, `_transit_where`, `holdings_basis`, `_on_trade_dates`, `_trade_pool_cost`, `_base_on_trade_dates`; `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `_t1135_cash_doc`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_fx_cash_v2_doc`; `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_t1135.py` — `load_cash`, `cash_mismatch`, `cash_key`, `cash_rows`, `cash_coins`, `_cash_acts`, `total_settled`; `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `d_t1135`: the cash an account holds outside Canada (a crypto account's, one whose `custodian_country` is abroad), a row per account and currency of category 1 at its cost day by day (the FX-on-cash ledger v2's pooled cost; Canadian dollars where the books give the balance), passed to taxjson-t1135 as `--cash FILE`, its days on the project's tax_date basis as the walk's (backlog TJ-0627); a cost the ledger cannot give leaves the total not settled (tax-logic CA-RPT-20; backlog TJ-0454, TJ-0609).
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_t1135.py` — `join_income_gains`, `paid_on_short`, `PIL_SHORT_WINDOW_DAYS`, `_gains_row_date`, `property_interest`, `cash_interest`, `check_gains_country`, `WrongCountryGains`, `foreign_isin_symbols`: the INCOME and GAIN(LOSS) columns (the gains files' dividends dated when paid, the base books' interest on a property, and the interest an account's cash earns as its cash row's, the ledger's holdings `interest` naming the currency: tax-logic CA-RPT-14; backlog TJ-0628), the refusal of a US-engine gains file, and the shares on a Canadian listing whose ISIN names another country (named for a `T1135` line).
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_t1135.py` — `property_category`, `load_categories`, `CATEGORY_NAMES`, `_cat`: each property's T1135 category (CAT); `src/taxjson/lib/tobase_map.py` — `t1135_kinds`, `NOT_SHARE_KINDS`: the interlisted master's units and funds, left for the user; `src/taxjson/lib/ticker_map.py` — `t1135_category`, `add_side_rule`: the optional CATEGORY of a `T1135` line.

- `src/taxjson/lib/cra_slips.py` — `parse_slips`, `parse_text`, `read_pdf`, `pdf_paths`, `broker_of_issuer`, `issuer_aliases`, `alias_of_issuer`, `book_groups`, `_bare_groups`, `place`, `drop_duplicates`, `plan_import`, `table`: T5 / T3 PDFs downloaded from CRA My Account (`slip-audit --import-cra`, `src/taxjson/cli/slips.py` — `_slip_audit_import_cra`): read with pdftotext (`pdf_text`: the absolute path after `--`, its text capped at `PDF_TEXT_MAX` and one time limit on reading and exit, `_read_capped`; each slip from its own slip line: the slip line and box rows only), a T5's broker from its issuer (the broker's whole name, or a carrying dealer the shipped `src/taxjson/data/slip_issuers.toml` names; broker accounts with no income rows from the parse metadata, `src/taxjson/lib/slip_audit.py` — `broker_account_files`), placed in an account, broker account and fund by the books' payments, each slip once (a duplicate dropped, an amended slip replacing its original of the same currency, one with only another currency's original said, `_other_currency_notes`: backlog TJ-5043), written as [[slip]] tables with a salted broker key (`src/taxjson/lib/slip_audit.py` — `key_salt`, `broker_key`, `resolve_keys`, `comment_out_tables`); `tjs redact` replaces the key in its copy (`src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_redact.py` — `_redact_broker_keys`).

`engine_net_gain` (Canada: the project year's net is the RETURN row the wrapper passes as `--return-row`, `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `_project_return_row`, `_return_row_totals`); `locked_years` (the locks are read before the gains run: US-WASH-22's add reaching a locked year is booked in the loss's year); `excluded_unknown_cost`, `incomplete_years`, `_no_rows` (the unknown-cost sales every pass left out, the US crypto pass's too); `own_year` (a closed project year's own lock gives its APPLIED and balance), `claims_over_gain` (a claim above its year's net gain), `applied_over_balance` (a lock that applied more than the record before it carries in), `rebuilt_balance` (the books' balance beside a lock's); `yearly_nets` (US: a §1256 contract's P/L split 40/60 into NET ST / NET LT), `section_1256_unsplit` (a lock closed before the split); Canada's question about the years it rebuilds: `_canada_ledger`, `_Readings` (the two readings; `_by_books`: what a typed balance would say on the nets, a hint beside the question, never a reading; `not_in_nets`: a balance and `carried_back` entries the nets don't reconcile), `_Said` (which `[carryover] carried_back` entries and recorded answers are taken: `take`, `others`, `check_rows`; `lock_stated`, an answer an earlier close recorded), and which claimed years a carry-back went to: `_orders` with `_walk`, `_options`, `_held_for` and `_short` (every reading of the years with no split on record, folded; those a later lock rules out dropped; `carry_back_split_cant_tell` where the project year's figures differ among them; `MAX_READINGS`), `moves` (an entry is folded before it is set aside), `back_to` and `_claim_order` (a split on record: a by-target entry, `_stated_backs`, or a lock's `carried_back_to`, `_canada_lock_carry`, `_recorded_splits`), `_own_split_checked` (the project year's own entry), with the sentences in `src/taxjson/lib/carry_back.py` — `cant_tell_text`, `_by_books_text`, `NOT_IN_NETS`, `not_in_nets_text`, `split_cant_tell`, `_split_cant_tell_text`, `reading_when`, `trim_to`, and the lock's record of the years, `lock_split`, `split_proposed_said` (called from `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `_split_for_lock`, `_carryforwards_summary`); the entry's parse: `src/taxjson/lib/project_tables.py` — `carried_back_entries`, `_back_targets`; handoff's notes: `src/taxjson/lib/carryforward.py` — `_order_notes`. What it reads besides the books: `src/taxjson/lib/carryover_inputs.py` — `load_claimed`, `us_lock_gains`, `us_lock_1256` (a US lock's Form 8949 lines with its Form 6781 line as its carryover took it).
- `src/taxjson/lib/carryforward.py` — `resolve_losses`, `resolve_amt`, `amt_from_config`, `record_block`, `lock_block` (through `filed_locks.read_lock`, with the project's country), `handoff_issues`, `partial_lock`: carry-forwards from one year to the next (from a lock taken before its year ended: said provisional); the block a lock carries is checked by `src/taxjson/lib/filed_locks.py` — `block_problem` (a present section must carry its closing balance; no negative balance; `_by_year_problem`: one entry per year of origin, 0 or more; the minimum tax closing the sum of its years).
- `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `cmd_form_export`, `cmd_t1135`, `cmd_carryover`, `_taxable_gains_argv`; `src/taxjson/cli/slips.py` — `cmd_reconcile_slips`, `cmd_slip_audit`: the commands.

## Comparing runs

`compare` diffs `reports/` with a snapshot: the run before (kept by every
full run), one saved by name, or the reports as filed. `snapshot` copies the
whole project; `clean` removes what a run wrote.

- `src/taxjson/lib/compare.py` — `take_full`, `_full_sources`, `_copy_tree`, `_links_leaving`, `_full_dir`, `FULL`; `src/taxjson/lib/project_layout.py` — `snapshots_folder`, `years_layout`; `src/taxjson/cli/books.py` — `cmd_snapshot`: `tjs snapshot`, the whole project copied to `snapshot/<year>.<timestamp>/` with its layout; `compare` finds one by name after `work/compare/` (load, `project` in its record naming the folder of its reports).
- `src/taxjson/lib/clean.py` — `projects`, `_overlap_problem`, `_places`, `held`, `plan`, `known`, `Known`, `work_ours`, `report_ours`, `never_removed`, `WORK_FILES`, `ACCOUNT_WORK`, `GROUP_WORK`, `BLEND_WORK`, `REPORT_FILES`, `ACCOUNT_REPORTS`, `PARSE_GROUPS`, `FIRST`, `PARTIAL_BOOKS`, `remove`, `kept_why`, `holds_books`, `no_books`, `_plan_work`, `_plan_reports`, `_plan_exports`, `_exports_target`, `CleanError`, `Busy`; `src/taxjson/cli/books.py` — `cmd_clean`, `_clean_report`; `src/taxjson/lib/project_layout.py` — `exports_record`, `EXPORTS_MANIFEST`; `src/taxjson/cli/main.py` — `_YEARS_ROOT_CMDS`: `tjs clean`, what a run generated removed (in work/ and reports/ what it recognises as taxjson's by full name: the records' names and the names the writers give a project's, an account's and a blend's files, but what no run rebuilds and never a .tt; the export its record names), refused when a place of the user's files of any year cleaned is in the work/ or reports/ of any year cleaned (an account's holdings read as sanity reads them: `src/taxjson/project/accounts.py` — `holdings_paths`), each project's run lock held first; the reports' .sum files and the run's record of what it read removed first, the books the views' banner looks for last (`src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_run_state_problems`), the locks after everything; in the folder holding the year folders, every year. The commands that refuse without books ask lib/clean's holds_books (and no_books), an account's book by the same names, before they read work/: `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `has_books`; `src/taxjson/cli/maps.py` — `cmd_ticker_map`; `src/taxjson/cli/slips.py` — `cmd_slip_audit`; `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `cmd_div_estimate`.
- `src/taxjson/lib/compare.py` — `take`, `_publish`, `_HIDDEN`, `rotate_previous`, `partial_accounts`, `full_run_done`, `PARTIAL_MARK` (a `run --account` since the last full run: `prev` stays that full run's), `save`, `load`, `_check_snapshot`, `list_snapshots`, `compare`, `render`, `render_list`, `report_files`, `normalized`, `aggregates`, `filed_reports_dir`, `FIGURES`, `PREV`, `FILED`: the snapshots (`work/compare/<name>/`; staged in a dot-named folder of the call's own, the earlier snapshot moved aside and back when the rename fails; a snapshot's shape checked when loaded), the comparison (undecodable bytes kept distinct) and its views; `src/taxjson/cli/books.py` — `cmd_compare`: the command, and the rotation at the start of `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_cmd_run` (in `_run_folders`; `full_run_done` at a full run's end); `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `fingerprint_delta`, `input_fingerprint`; `src/taxjson/lib/fingerprint_record.py` — `recorded_fingerprint`: what a snapshot records the reports were built from and how two fingerprints are compared.

## Filed-year lock and year hand-off

`close-year` snapshots a filed year's figures; every later run and
`check-filed` recompute the year and report drift. `handoff` records what a
closed year carried forward and checks that the next year's project starts
from exactly that.

- `src/taxjson/lib/channels.py` — `running_build`, `build_label`, `same_build`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_closed_with`: the taxjson a lock was closed with (its `taxjson` record), named on a drift.
- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_filed.py` — `write_snapshot`, `recompute_year`, `diff_snapshot` (a figure a cent or less from the lock's is no drift: `_TOL_CENTS`, in whole cents), `lock_for_year`; reading a lock, `src/taxjson/lib/filed_locks.py` — `project_locks`, `aggregates_from_gains` (the rows held to their types, `check_row_types`, before its views read them: lib/compare passes the books past the row funnel), `lock_shape_problem`, `finite_num` (a locked figure that is not a finite number is a damaged lock, in the shape check and the drift check), `partial_year_note`, `income_as_summed` (the income close-year and `years` show: `sum`'s taxable SUBTOTAL, each account's cents): the filed-year lock.
- `src/taxjson/lib/filed_locks.py` — `read_lock`, `LockProblem`, `lock_damage`, `load_lock_json`, `file_year`, `lock_country_problem` (`prior`: the advice of a next-year reader), `filed_rows_problem` (`carried`: a lock whose filed dispositions hold another year's rows, refused by the readers of what it carried — estimate, carryover, handoff — naming `REFILE_DISPOSITIONS`; the rows, `filed_rows_outside`), `block_problem` (the `carryforwards` block), `missing_lock_problem` / `missing_prior_lock` (a closed year's deleted lock, the freeze note still in its taxjson.toml: refused like a damaged one, never read as no record): the one check of a lock every reader applies — check-filed and the run's drift check, `years`, the next year's carryover, estimate / amt / close-year and handoff — so a lock one refuses is never sound to another; `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_check_filed_years`; `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `cmd_amt`; `src/taxjson/lib/years_report.py` — `years_report`; `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_carryover.py` — `lock_figure`; `src/taxjson/lib/carryforward.py` — `lock_block`; `src/taxjson/lib/handoff.py` — `validate_record`, `load_record`: its readers.
- `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `cmd_close_year` (all or nothing, TJ-4627: `_close_year` holds the steps, the freeze, the reports as filed and the lock one step with the lock written last; `_not_closed` what is said before the lock, `_closed_anyway` the warning after it; its parts: `_close_year_refusals`, `_close_year_checks`, `_close_year_extra`, `_close_year_report`; the step, `src/taxjson/lib/write_step.py` — `WriteStep` (each write's undo step added before it, the lock its commit file: `file_id` tells it was written, `settle` runs the undo to the end, `hold_interrupts` has a Ctrl-C pressed again wait for it); an input's own line, `src/taxjson/lib/cli_diag.py` — `input_error_text`), `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `_close_year_attention` (the checklist steps before the lock that need attention: asked on a terminal, refused without one unless `--yes`), `_replaced_totals_moved` (what `--force` moved from the replaced lock's totals: money on any cent, in whole cents, since that lock is check-filed's reference; the count on any change), `cmd_check_filed`; `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_refuse_books_older_than_inputs`, `_books_older_than_inputs`, `_rebuild_first` (close-year, check-filed and form-export refuse books older than the inputs, by the last full run's record, and so do t1135, sum and estimate: `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `cmd_t1135`, `_refuse_t1135_books`, `_t1135_reads_cash`; `src/taxjson/cli/estimate.py` — `_refuse_sum_books`, owner decision 2026-11-18, TJ-4347; `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `inputs_changed`, `_locks_from_these_books`, `_lock_ids`, `_closed_from`, `book_hashes` (a lock closed from these books since, of the year read or a later one, is no change: `read_upto`, its frozen inputs the run's and its frozen `books` hashes the books' now, which close-year records; one removed, replaced, put back after a run without it, or closed from other books is, TJ-4308, TJ-4311), books with no record (TJ-4309) or built by another taxjson (`src/taxjson/lib/fingerprint_record.py` — `books_code`, the package fingerprint a full run records, TJ-4310), or a pass across accounts an `--account` run left behind; the elections first when the last run deferred; exit 1, no --force; close-year among its own refusals; held by `tests/features/run/test_filing_commands_refuse_books_older_than_the_inputs.py` — `TestFilingCommandsRefuseBooksOlderThanTheInputs`); `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_check_filed_years`; `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `_carryforwards_for_lock`, `_as_filed` (the net capital loss from `--filed-dispositions`), `_books_loss_not_carried` (a first year's lock carrying no loss the books carry in); `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `_carry_back_for_handoff`; `src/taxjson/cli/estimate.py` — `_carry_back_or_warn`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_carry_in_moved` (an opening a cent or less from the record's closing is the same balance, in whole cents), `_carry_in_detail`; `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `_later_locks_on` (a filed year built on the net capital loss of an earlier record closed again since: check-filed of that year, the earlier year's re-close); `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_locked_year_flags`; `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `cmd_handoff`, `_prior_record_path`, `_handoff_gains_flags`: `close-year`, `check-filed` and `handoff`, and the drift check every run makes of each filed year.
- `src/taxjson/lib/handoff.py` — `snapshot`, `check`, `render`, `validate_record`, `straddlers`, `dispositions`, `_lots_by_symbol`, `_gain_rows`, `load_filed_dispositions`: the year-to-year record and its check (`snapshot`: the engine's own Dec 31 pools, read from the cost timeline of one full-history run per group — `src/taxjson/lib/cost_timeline.py` — `inventory_on` — with its `deferred_wash` tally; a US crypto group without the wash-sale rule; a filed disposition must be of the year closed). The Dec-31 rows (`boundary_rows`, compared by `_boundary_issues` and `_boundary_section`) keep each row's base `net` and `income` and its amounts as the broker stated them (`_Native`, `_Stated`: a converted row found by its id in work/<account>_raw.json; a crypto row's from _filled.json unless the fill stage priced it; a dividend's same-day TAX rows as `withheld` in their own currencies, or the day's as `day` where dividends share them, compared once by `_day_withheld`; `boundary_rows` raises BooksError when a converted row's native book is missing). `_HereBooks` holds this project's rows (`pairs`: unchanged pairs first, then within the tolerance); `_same_row` identifies a row within the 0.5% tolerance (`_near`), `_space` / `_changed` / `_moved` tell a correction to the cent in each stated currency (`_c`: cents half up), which `_correction` reports for amendment, `_in_window` is which rows are compared (sales and income from Dec 30), `_lone_and_gone` pairs a row here and a record row re-dated, re-counted or re-spelled into one item (`_redated`; re-dated past Dec 31, an item to amend: `_moved_out`) and reports the rest: `_neither`, and `_removed` for a counted row the books no longer hold where the account's broker exports reach its day (`_HereBooks.first`, `_exported`: never a .tt file's row, an OPENING line or a row taxjson made), and `_rough` / `_rough_note` count the rows compared only within the tolerance (backlog TJ-5065, TJ-5066, TJ-5180, TJ-5181, TJ-5183); a record without native amounts is compared at its net, with a note. The double-report loop and its `own_closed` / `_is_own` replay skip a sale another of this project's accounts recorded (backlog TJ-5067, as `_found` does). A US record keeps each lot held at Dec 31 too (`year_end_lots`: `_lots_by_symbol`, from the US engine's `lots_at`, `taxjson-gains --lots-at`, `src/taxjson/engine/us/prepare.py` — `_us_lot_rows`), compared lot by lot (`_same_lots`, `_same_seq`, `_lots_why`; backlog TJ-0554, TJ-0919). The gains files' rows (`dispositions`, the check's double-report loop) and a US run's `lots_at` (`_lots_by_symbol`) are read through model/result (step 6c).

`_later_locks`; a readable year folder named before a broken copy: TJ-5088); `_settings_back` and `_thawed`, taxjson.toml's text with only the close's own lines edited: under a note at most what the freeze wrote, `_commented_setting`, and with the lock's `frozen` record only a key it names with that value, so a commented line of the person's is never activated or removed; a setting whose line is gone or another's written from the record, `RECORD_GONE`, `RECORD_OTHER`; what was frozen is what the record names, and without one what a note's own line restored; `_input_copies` and `_tobase_copy`, each frozen copy sorted by `_against_shared` into removed (the shared file's bytes), kept in the backup (`DIFFERS`, `ONLY_COPY`, `UNREADABLE`, `GENERATED`) or left (a link, or reached through a linked folder: `_through_link`); `src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `input_files`, `fingerprint_changes`, `frozen_files`, `run_config_state`, `run_config_keys_changed` (the frozen record holds files only, never the US locked-years entry), `_same_tobase`, `_fp_key`, `_inputs_folder_name` (a shared `inputs/` keyed as the setting names it, a symlink included, so the frozen copy's keys match): the files the freeze copies and the comparison of a lock's frozen hashes with the project now.

## Redact

`redact` strips account numbers, names and contact details from a broker
export while keeping every row's shape, so a file can be shared as a parser
sample or a bug report. It is a best-effort pattern matcher.

- `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_redact.py` — `redact_file`, `redact_text`, `redact_tree`, `compile_patterns`, `Pseudonyms`, `load_denylist`, `identity_counts` (`IDENTITY_KINDS`: every kind a consumer such as the generate-parser gate counts): the redactor, one CSV record at a time (`_csv_records`: a quoted cell may span lines; a quote that never closes is read line by line and listed, `UNBALANCED_REASON`; a tab-, semicolon- or pipe-delimited table's identity cells first, `_redact_delimited_identity`; a generic export's lines above its header blanked whole first, `_blank_preamble`, the count `_preamble`'s, for a file detection reads as generic, through generic's `preamble_records`); account-id placeholders `_known_placeholder` (never an id of the input: `IdTable.reserved`, filled for a project copy by `_tree_ids`) replaced in one pass by `_id_subber` (`redact_text`, `redact_project_text`, `_sweep`); wallet addresses `_WALLET` (pseudonymised when `_plausible_wallet`), leftovers `_WALLETISH` / `_wallet_like` (review); the review pass `_review` (`_caps_name`, `_LAST_FIRST`, `_from_to_name`: a bare `FROM NAME` / `TO NAME`); ids after a broker name `_BROKER_ACCOUNT`; a year's own taxjson.toml, ticker.map and tobase.map in the project copy: `redact_project_text` (the configuration's ids from `config_ids`, comments, a folder path of this computer in a comment `_redact_comment_paths`, denylist / `--also` matches; the copied taxjson.toml must read, `_valid_copy_config`; its escaped strings written plain first, `plain_toml_strings`); the close-year record a year folder's copy carries for its estimate, `_prior_record` (in `prior/`, `PRIOR_DIR`), its folder paths of this computer replaced by `redact_record_paths` (`_record_path`, `PATH_PLACEHOLDER`); every redacted export read again by its parser and its numbers compared with the original's, `numbers_changed` (an option's strike is no house number, `_sub_address`); the project copy walks `inputs/` with `_walk_inputs` (a file link leaving it is skipped, `_inside`); in a year folder of a shared-exports project `_project_tree` (with `_PROJECT_FILES`) builds that year as a single-folder project, a link in the shared inputs allowed within the folder holding the years.
- `src/taxjson/cli/tools.py` — `cmd_redact`: `tjs redact`.

## Configuration: taxjson.toml, init, format and migrate

taxjson.toml holds `[settings]` (country, year, tax date basis ...) and one
table per account. Every reader goes through the same checks. `init` writes
a new project from a template; `format` re-renders an existing file into the
template keeping the user's values; `migrate` folds an old project's separate
files into ticker.map and taxjson.toml.

- `src/taxjson/project/toml_io.py` — `parse_toml`, `read_toml`, `read_toml_text`, `decode_toml_bytes` (the one encoding, BOM and newline policy, for the text parsed and the text edited back), `TOML_ERRORS` (the one TOML parser: the standard library's `tomllib`).
- `src/taxjson/project/accounts.py` — `AccountSettings`, `EstimateSettings`, `accounts_of`, `account_of`, `typed_accounts`, `typed_account`, `configured_accounts`, `sheltered_by_name`, `estimate_of`, `typed_estimate` (typed views beside the [accounts.*] and [estimate] tables, which stay dicts: they are written, dumped and handed on), `_transfers_accounts` (the accounts whose TRANSFER rows are in the books); `src/taxjson/project/settings.py` — `TypedTable` (the frozen table the three share: attributes, `has`, `empty`, `present`, `extra`, `as_dict`; no mapping since TJ-1635), `Settings`, `KEYS`, `from_table`, `replace`, `as_dict`, `has`, `empty`, `as_settings` (the [settings] table typed, built once by the readers: `src/taxjson/project/settings.py` — `settings_of`; the helpers under `lib/` take theirs through as_settings); `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `load_config` (one body; `missing_ok`: the query wrappers' read, {} with no taxjson.toml), `load_settings`, `validate_config`, `_normalize_settings`; `src/taxjson/cli/summaries.py` — `_warn_config_tables`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `_refuse_bad_account_types`; `src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `cmd_init` (a folder made for the project is made owner-only; `_init_config_text`: a read-only taxjson.toml refused, the config's text; `_init_scaffold`: taxjson.toml written and the other files only when absent, a new project's files taken back when a write fails; `_init_account_notes`; `_init_next_details`: the --details next steps), `_year_accounts` (an earlier year added beside year folders: no template folder the shared inputs/ lacks, the note names their accounts), `_render_init_config`, `_refuse_init_in_year_folder`, `_init_filing_hint`, `_sibling_grant_since`, `cmd_format`, `_backup_config`; `src/taxjson/cli/migrate.py` — `cmd_migrate`, `_migrate_steps`, `_migrate_plan`, `_migrate_done_note`: reading and checking taxjson.toml; init (refused in an empty folder named like a year; from January to April a line for filing last year; `src/taxjson/cli/file.py` — `option_grant_timing_since` left commented unless another year folder sets it); format; migrate (every phase — the project's old files, missing_history.json, the shared tobase.map — planned before the first writes; a later refusal names what an earlier phase did).
- `src/taxjson/lib/demo.py` — `ACCOUNTS`, `SCENARIOS`, `files`, `data_dir`, `settings`; `src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `_init_demo`: `tjs init --demo`, a Canadian 2024 year folder of made-up exports (`src/taxjson/data/demo/<account>/`, the broker demos of `examples/`, a LIRA (Questrade) and an RRSP (IB) of its own, plus an IB statement with the getting-started scenarios and its saved spin-off election in `manifest.json`).
- `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_grant_since_warning`, `_books_write_options`, `_grant_since_after_year`: the run's warnings about `option_grant_timing_since` (not set while the books hold a written option; later than `year`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/config_check.py` — `settings_problems`, `account_type_problems`, `account_name_problem`, `invalid_account_name`, `ACCOUNT_TYPES`, `ACCOUNT_KEYS` (re-exported from `src/taxjson/project/accounts.py` — `ACCOUNT_KEYS`, `ESTIMATE_KEYS`, the one source), `RETIRED_SETTINGS`: the checks every config reader applies.
- `src/taxjson/lib/config_template.py` — `SETTINGS_SPEC`, `ACCOUNT_SPEC`, `TABLES`, `render_init`, `format_config`, `scaffold_document`, `Key`: every key taxjson reads, documented per country.
- `src/taxjson/lib/migrate.py` — `plan`, `apply`, `Plan`, `legacy_files`, `LEGACY_FILES`, `MigrateError`: moving old files into the new places.
- `src/taxjson/lib/safe_write.py` — `write_user_file`, `write_atomic`, `atomic_open`, `backup_copy` (each `.bak` name taken with an exclusive create, so overlapping callers never share one), `file_lock` (a link at the lock name replaced, `LockLinkError` when it cannot be), `OutsideLinkError`: writes that never follow a planted symlink, each through a temp file of its own (overlapping writers never share one; `_create` makes every new file, owner-only, its descriptor owned by the file object, and atomic_open sets up the temp's removal before the temp exists: TJ-4893), with a backup; `file_lock` serializes a read-modify-write. Generated state in `work/` goes through `write_atomic`; `tests/features/configuration/test_generated_state_never_follows_links.py` lists the reviewed direct writes left in the core. `write_changed`, `holds`: the run's bookkeeping is written only when it changed (the records, work/.deps/run.json, the stamps, a stage's captured stderr: `src/taxjson/pipeline/tools.py` — `run_to_file`, `_write_work_stamp`; TJ-4962); every write is fsync'd before its rename.
- `src/taxjson/lib/requires.py` — `FEATURES`, `INIT_FEATURES`, `problem`, `features_of`, `raised`, `raised_text`, `raise_requirement`, `lowers`; `src/taxjson/cli/main.py` — `_refuse_newer_projects`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `_refuse_newer_config`; `src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `_init_requires`; `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_raise_requirements`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `_unknown_setting_warnings`: `[settings] requires_taxjson`, the oldest taxjson a project runs on — refused by every command of an older one before anything else; FEATURES lists the release that first reads each layout feature (one line per layout change), and init, new-year, migrate, update-tobase-map, find-missing-history and align raise the key for what they write, never lowering it. An unknown `[settings]` key is one warning line in every command.

## One folder of exports for every year

`init` (by default) writes inputs/<account>/ shared by year folders, each a
complete project whose `[settings] inputs_dir` / `holdings_dir` /
`exports_dir` name the shared folders, and in Canada `tobase_map` the
tobase.map beside the year folders that every year reads. Every input
path goes through lib/project_layout; each year keeps its own
taxjson.toml and ticker.map (`compare` compares the `tobase_map`
setting, and counts tobase.map lines only of per-year copies, the layout
before v0.27.1).

- `src/taxjson/lib/project_layout.py` — `parse_config_text` (taxjson.toml's text parsed, LayoutError on bad TOML), `inputs_dir`, `shared_inputs`, `ticker_map_path`, `data_file`, `slips_dir`, `holdings_folder`, `exports_folder`, `project_path`, `write_boundary`, `folder_setting`, `setting_problems` (a folder leaving the folder that holds the project; a year folder holding another year; a year folder holding its own tobase.map beside the shared one, `tobase_both_problem`), `tobase_setting`, `tobase_map_path`, `shared_tobase`, `tobase_shown`, `tobase_layout`, `project_map_path`, `exports_overlap` (an `exports_dir` overlapping the inputs, holdings, a year folder or the project's own folders), `unconfigured_inputs`, `local_input_folders`, `project_of_input`, `is_account_folder` (the project an input file belongs to: the one `TAXJSON_PROJECT_ROOT` names, which `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `load_config` sets for every stage, when the file is in its shared inputs folder), `year_dirs`, `multi_root`, `new_year_text`, `own_folder_value` (a `holdings_dir` inside the year's own folder, as another year writes it), `compare` (`flat_paths`, `account_order`, `map_rules`: an unreadable file is an error), `set_key_text` (every taxjson.toml edit: inline tables, dotted keys, arrays of tables; the result read back with `parse_config_text` and compared with `_with_key`, else LayoutError), `toml_statements` (a taxjson.toml's tables and keys by line, quoted names and values over several lines included): where a project's files are, and the year-folder tools; `src/taxjson/lib/years_report.py` — `years_report`: `tjs years`' report of each year project (a year's tobase.map: `_year_tobase`).
- `src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `_single_folder_project`, `_single_folder_hint` (a project folder that is no year folder: `years` / `new-year` point at `migrate --to-years`), `cmd_years`, `_years_table`, `_years_shared_moved` (a filed year's shared exports changed since the close: `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_shared_inputs_moved` per year — `exports`: its rows gained, changed or lost, counted, and a new file in one of `_frozen_year_accounts`' shared folders, each file read as the parsers read it (`decode_broker_text`: UTF-16 too) and each row dated by `_line_year` (`_LINE_DATE_RES`); `mappings`: a generic mapping that reads one of its CSVs otherwise (`generic_mapping` on both sides, compared as parsed TOML); `unread`: a file it could not compare, named (`src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `shared_not_compared`, INPUTS `shared unchecked`); `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_shared_exports_moved` is its `exports` — and `_shared_decisions_moved` — `_decisions_moved`, a per-entry comparison of the shared `manifest.json` / `sends.json` with the year's copy, also named by `_check_filed_years`; the shared folder they compare with: `_frozen_shared_src`, the lock's `inputs_dir`, else for a year closed as a single folder and then migrated the inputs/ beside the year folders); `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_year_dir_arg` (the `-C` path of a year folder its `! ` lines name, from where the command ran); `src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `_years_tobase` (the tobase.map lines of both views: shared, per-year copies, a filed year's frozen copy), `cmd_new_year` (`_new_year_text`: the new year's taxjson.toml text from the previous year's, a default grant-timing cutoff kept, a frozen year's shared folders named again, `_implicit_grant_since`; `_new_year_holdings`: the snapshots folder to make inside the year, or the link to a shared one; `_new_year_publish`: made in a staging folder, moved into place when complete, each move's take-back (`_moved_back`) added before it; `_new_year_place`: the moves as one `WriteStep`, its taxjson.toml the commit file, `_new_year_stopped` saying what was made; `_templated`: the copied config in the template layout; `_new_year_details`: the `--details` form), `cmd_align` (a filed year's warning, said before anything is asked or written: `_align_filed_warning`; every edit made and checked in memory before any file is written, `_align_texts`, the file as it would be written by the config check and the folder settings' check: `_align_new_config_problems`; an account only one year has listed whole, never brought over: `_align_lone_accounts`; `_align_write`: the writes as one `WriteStep`, a file that cannot be written refused before any is, each write's undo (`_align_undo`: the file put back, the backup its write made removed, `_backups_of`) added before it, `_align_stopped` saying what was and was not written; `_align_write_result`: the `--write --all --json` result, printed after the writes), `_years_folder`; `src/taxjson/cli/main.py` — `_refuse_years_root`, `_YEARS_ROOT_CMDS`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `_refuse_folder_settings`; `src/taxjson/cli/migrate.py` — `_migrate_to_years` (`_to_years_preflight`: taxjson.toml and .gitignore checked before anything moves); `src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_write_exports`, `_EXPORT_PATTERNS`, `_exports_manifest`, `_EXPORTS_MANIFEST` (the files the last export wrote; only those are replaced); `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_shared_inputs_note` (a file written into the shared exports applies to every year; `_frozen_years_apart` names the frozen filed years it does not reach); `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_sends_tt_shown`, `_sends_json_shown` (where the crypto-sends files are): `years`, `new-year`; align, the refusal in the folder holding the years, `migrate --to-years` and the newest year's exports/; `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_redact.py` — `_project_tree`, `_copy_holdings_lists` (a year folder redacted as a runnable single-folder project).
- `src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `_years_as_filed` (a filed year's differences from the newest year are its own: `(filed)`, never a step), `_years_act` (an `! ` line within 100 columns, cut at a clause or word), `_align_filed_year` (`align --write` in a filed year warns first): `years` and `align` around a filed year.
- `src/taxjson/lib/holdings_dir.py` — `discover`, `listed_files`, `folder_for`, `broker_ids`, `snapshot_files`, `report_kind`, `unread_notes`: the year's holdings/ snapshots `sanity` finds by `[meta] broker_account`, `[meta] account` or file name (a snapshot of an account holding nothing: `[meta]` alone, `src/taxjson/lib/positions_reports.py` — `empty_snapshot`; a broker's RBC Holdings Export or IB statement there is read too, claimed by its name; any other file is named as not read) (`src/taxjson/pipeline/sanity.py` — `_sanity_items_from_config`); `src/taxjson/lib/holdings_dir.py` — `where_used`, `in_use`: the positions files `sanity` compares and where they are, which the run's closing list then names (`src/taxjson/pipeline/run.py` — `_first_run_summary`, never in run_summary.json; `src/taxjson/lib/first_run.py` — `HOLDINGS_TO_COMPARE`, `holdings_detail`, `_heading`) and the checklist's step asks too (`src/taxjson/lib/checklist.py` — `d_sanity`).

## The Canada and USA partition

Canadian and US rules never mix. One module resolves a project's country
(`src/taxjson/rules/country.py`, a leaf, re-exported by lib/country.py); one
owns the tables saying which settings, config tables, flags, commands and
project files belong to which country. `scripts/check_tax_rules.py` checks
that the tables are complete.

- `src/taxjson/rules/country.py` — `canonical_country`, `CountryError`, `check_engine_allowed`; `src/taxjson/lib/country.py` — `settings_country`, `SETTING_COUNTRY`, `CONFIG_COUNTRY`, `FLAG_COUNTRY`, `COMMAND_COUNTRY`, `PROJECT_FILE_COUNTRY`, `config_country_problems`, `refuse_foreign_flags`, `default_tax_date`, `basis_pooled_across_accounts`, `lot_rule`: the one country resolver; the ownership tables (settings, config tables, flags, commands, project files); the checks built on them; per-country defaults.
- `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `_country`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/inputs.py` — `_tax_date`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `_is_us`; `src/taxjson/cli/common.py` — `_refuse_other_country_books`: how the CLI reads them.

## Markets, calendars and dates

Venue suffixes, currencies and the security lists taxjson cannot read from an
export live in one data file, overridable from ticker.map. Settlement dates
use each market's holiday calendar and the T+3, T+2, T+1 history.

- `src/taxjson/lib/markets.py` — `overrides`, `suffix_currency`, `is_canadian_listing`, `split_share_roots`, `contract_size`, `option_contract_size` (an option's MULT: the contract, the underlying without its listing, its root), `usd_unit_listing`, `crypto_venue_suffix`: market reference data (and the convention for a Canadian-listed fund's US-dollar units; a crypto exchange's venue suffix in the holdings handoff). The shipped data's reader and the suffixes it names sit below markets and the ticker.map reader: `src/taxjson/lib/markets_data.py` — `data`, `known_suffixes`, `suffix_of`, `canadian_suffixes`, `listing_suffix_re`.
- `src/taxjson/lib/symbol_case.py` — `book_symbol`, `class_spelling`, `record`, `fold_rows`, `folded`, `collisions`, `collision_message`, `SPELLINGS_KEY`: an export's symbol in the books' spelling (upper case; a spelling a ticker.map `DISTINCT` case pair keeps apart, its class spelling), applied where a parse's rows leave it (`src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/base.py` — `parse_file`, `upper_case_symbols`) and to every corporate-action event (`src/taxjson/lib/corp_extract.py` — `extract_events`, `map_tables`, `project_corp_case`) and holdings file (`src/taxjson/lib/positions_reports.py` — `_read_ib`, `_read_toml`); each output keeps the spellings it read (`symbol_spellings` in its metadata), and two that differ only in case stop the run (`src/taxjson/pipeline/stages.py` — `spellings_checked`, `_checked_outputs`: the parses before the corporate actions are read; `src/taxjson/bin/taxjson_corp_actions.py` — `_read_events`: the corporate actions before an election is read or saved; `src/taxjson/lib/symbol_case.py` — `refusal`) until a `GLOBAL` or `DISTINCT` case pair decides them (`src/taxjson/lib/ticker_map_rules.py` — `_case_pair`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/ca_listing.py` — `canonical_ca_listing`, `canonical_ca_root`, `is_ca_pref_series`, `book_listing`: the one spelling of a Canadian listing in the books (ROOT.TO for every Canadian venue, a `.V` only in CAD; a preferred series dotted), which every parser (`src/taxjson/lib/brokerages/base.py` — `apply_currency_suffix`), the `.tt` reader and a ticker.map EXTRACT target (`src/taxjson/lib/ticker_map.py` — `extract_symbol`) give a symbol, and every other ticker.map line's symbol (`book_listing`; a `.V` warned about where the books spell it ROOT.TO: `src/taxjson/lib/ticker_map_rules.py` — `_venture_spelling_warnings`); a leaf on the market data, below the ticker.map reader.
- `src/taxjson/data/markets.toml` — `venues`, `currency_suffix`, `isin_country_suffix`, `isin_domicile_suffix`, `usd_unit_class`, `ib_venues`, `kraken_assets`: the shipped defaults (an ISIN country's home market; a country that names a domicile and no market, whose suffix is only where an IB income row's listing is looked for first: `src/taxjson/lib/markets.py` — `isin_country_suffix`, `isin_domicile_suffix`).
- `src/taxjson/lib/market_calendar.py` — `add_settlement_days`, `is_settlement_day`, `is_trading_day`, `nyse_holidays`, `tsx_holidays`, `CA_USD`, `market_key`: settlement calendars (a joined key such as `CA_USD`, a US-dollar trade on a Canadian market, settles only on the days every calendar it names settles).
- `src/taxjson/lib/dates.py` — `settlement_date`, `settlement_lag_days`, `date_to_epoch`, `market_of`, `settlement_calendar`, `last_trade_date_settling_by`, `as_of_date_problem`: date helpers for the radar and planning views (a `--date` whose window leaves the calendar is refused); `settlement_calendar` is the calendar every parser's blank-settle fallback uses (the listing's market; the US days too for a US-dollar trade on a Canadian market).

## The fetch plugin

The core holds no broker API client and reads no broker credential.
`tjs fetch` finds installed fetchers through the `taxjson.fetchers` entry
point; the separate taxjson-fetch package provides Questrade and IBKR Flex
downloads.

- `src/taxjson/lib/fetchers.py` — `discover`, `Fetcher`, `FetchRequest`, `ENTRY_POINT_GROUP`, `add_fetcher_arguments`, `listing`: the core side of the plugin contract; `LOCKSTEP_DISTRIBUTION`, `lockstep_problem`, `mismatched`, `not_loaded`, `UPGRADE_HINT`: taxjson-fetch runs only at the core's own X.Y.Z, another version is never loaded; `tjs fetch --list` names it as not loaded, and a fetch it would serve refuses in one line (TJ-2554).
- `src/taxjson/pipeline/plugin_api.py` — `detect_broker`, `input_files`, `looks_like_ib_text`: where a plugin imports these of the core run (never taxjson.bin: `scripts/check_architecture.py` — `plugin_violations`); a published plugin's older routes through bin stay (`tests/features/cli/test_cli_out_of_the_entry_point_and_run_state.py` — `PUBLISHED_ROUTES`).
- `src/taxjson/cli/setup.py` — `cmd_fetch`, `_no_fetcher_exit`, `_stale_fetcher_exit`, `_fetch_epilog`; `src/taxjson/pipeline/config.py` — `_fetch_plugin_note`, `_fetch_brokerages`: `tjs fetch`.
- `packages/taxjson-fetch/src/taxjson_fetch/plugin.py` — `BrokerFetcher`: the object the core loads.
- `packages/taxjson-fetch/src/taxjson_fetch/command.py` — `run`, `_run`, `_run_locked`, `_merge_csv_text`, `_qt_drop_blank_copies`, `_Merged`, `_qt_merged`, `_qt_say_merged`, `_qt_write_merged`, `_qt_row_shown`, `_questrade_token_file`, `_qt_open_session`, `_questrade_token_write`, `_flex_lost_dates`, `_qt_window_overlap`, `_qt_trim_file`, `_qt_live_holdings`: the fetch command (downloads into `request.inputs`, the shared inputs folder of a multi-year project; `--positions` into the year's holdings folder; one per project under `work/.fetch.lock`, taken by `_run` around the read-merge-publish step itself, `_run_locked`; the token's read, refresh and save under a lock beside the token file; merging a re-fetch into the existing CSV, a row that differs from another only by a blank Symbol being that row's copy: the named one kept, a file that holds both repaired in one merge (the download's count decides; merged again, nothing changes), the rows dropped named and the file as it was kept as a `.bak`; `--trim-overlap` backups through `backup_copy`).
- `packages/taxjson-fetch/src/taxjson_fetch/api.py` — `qt_refresh`, `qt_activities`, `qt_blank_copies`, `_qt_base_and_symbol`, `qt_positions`, `flex_fetch`, `positions_to_holdings_toml`, `mask_account_number`, `scrub_detail`, `write_private`: the Questrade and Flex API clients (`scrub_detail` masks tokens and account ids in every broker error text), and the owner-only atomic writer of fetched files.
- `packages/taxjson-fetch/pyproject.toml` — `taxjson.fetchers`: the entry-point registration.

## Release channels and install

`main` is the development line; a `vX.Y.Z` tag is a release. channels.json
names the release each channel (stable, beta) points at. The installer clones
a channel's release; `release.sh --prepare` lands the version commit after
the full gate, and `release.sh` (the cut) tags it once GitHub CI passed on
it and pushes the tag to the private dev repo (`origin`); the publisher
publishes it to the public repo after its gate and wait, and the public
repo's `release.yml` makes the GitHub release; `promote.sh` moves a
channel and asks the publisher for it with a fast-lane tag; `land.sh` is how
both (and every change) reach the dev `main`, by pull request.
docs/releasing.md has the steps.

- `install.sh` — `main`, `newest`, `named`, `vernewer`, `is_release`, `--channel`: the one-line installer (`named`: channels.json on main read strictly, as `channels.parse_channels` does; a newer tag the install is on is kept only when `is_release`; an existing install is a clone or a linked worktree, `-e "$DIR/.git"`).
- `install.sh` — `DRY_RUN`, `DRY_TMP`, `mktemp -d`: `TAXJSON_DRY_RUN=1` resolves the channel and stops; with no install yet it clones into a temporary folder removed on exit, never `TAXJSON_DIR`.
- `install.sh` — `pip_locked`, `LOCKS`, `.locks-from-main`, `--require-hashes`, `--no-build-isolation`: every dependency from the hash-pinned locks (wheels only), then taxjson and taxjson-fetch `--no-deps` from the checkout; a hash mismatch stops, a missing wheel falls back to the pinned core.
- `requirements/fx.txt` — `inputs-sha256`, `body-sha256`, `--hash=sha256`: one lock per extra (and `build.txt`, `core.txt`), universal over platforms and Python 3.12+.
- `scripts/lock_deps.py` — `lock_inputs`, `compile_locks`, `check`, `coverage`, `report`, `PLATFORMS`, `PYTHONS`, `REQUIRED`, `ACCEPTED_GAPS`: regenerates the locks with uv, checks them against pyproject.toml (offline) and checks wheel coverage (network; a known gap in an optional lock is listed as accepted, any other is reported, and a required lock accepts none).
- `channels.json` — `stable`, `beta`: where each channel points.
- `scripts/kit_checks.py` — `CONFIG`, `SCRIPT_PATH`, `osv-scanner`, `opengrep`: the publish kit's dependency and code checks, an unchanged copy of the kit's `checks/kit_checks.py` (kit doctor compares the two); every hit blocks unless `kit-checks.toml` (`lock_files`, `code_exceptions`, `vuln_exceptions`) excepts it. Run by `.github/workflows/kit-checks.yml` on the dev repo's pull requests (`github.event.repository.private`, `taxjson-dev`) and by `kit preflight` before a tag.
- `scripts/release.sh` — `--prepare`, `## Unreleased`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `scripts/ci.sh`, `scan_notes`, `make_notes`, `publish_main`, `tag_free`, `github-ci.sh`, `release_edits_only`, `--fresh-gate`, `--gate-max-age`, `PUBLIC_REMOTE`: a release in two steps — `--prepare` (CHANGELOG heading, version bump in both packages, full gate — or a reused PASS of the same tree plus `ci.sh --release-edits` — the version commit, landed through `publish_main`), then the cut (the version commit found on main by content, green GitHub CI on its push, a tag name free on the public repo too, scanned notes, the tag pushed to the dev repo).
- `scripts/release-notes.sh` — `## $TAG`: a release's notes, the CHANGELOG section (release.sh scans them; the public repo's release.yml publishes them).
- `.github/workflows/release.yml` — `gh release create`, `release-notes.sh`, `github.repository == 'taxjson/taxjson'`: the GitHub release for a tag the publisher pushed, on the public repo only.
- `scripts/land.sh` — `gate`, `fail`, `TAXJSON_LAND_DIRECT`, `gh pr create`, `--auto --rebase`, `STRICT`, `AUTO_OK` (GitHub's auto-merge only once every check passed: it waits for the required ones alone), `expected`, `checks`, `origin_main`, `git pull -q --rebase --autostash`, `patch_ids`: lands local main's new commits through a pull request (the pre-push gate, a branch, rebase-merged once its checks pass, only onto the base it pushed on — GitHub's auto-merge only when main's rules require an up-to-date branch; a landing on top of other commits is named: the Actions jobs of the pushed commit's runs on that branch and its commit statuses, not `gh pr checks`; a pull-request workflow with no run yet is pending — `expected` reads the workflows of the pushed commit, `HEAD_SHA`, not the working tree; a request about the checks that fails is pending too; a failed check closes it, and a close GitHub refuses is said, never claimed done).
- `scripts/private-values` — `denylist`, `expand`, `private_json`, `email_allowlist`: the publisher's fingerprint provider (`kit values sync` runs it; it prints values): the denylist's finite lines and `private.json`'s addresses, without the owner's names, noreply addresses and allowlisted addresses (public). Tested by `tests/test_private_values.py`.
- `scripts/github-ci.sh` — `ask`, `--appear-wait`, `head_sha=$SHA&event=push&branch=main`, `actions/runs/$id`, `TAXJSON_CI_WAIT_MINS`: did tests.yml pass on the push of this commit to main (release cut and promote); a run still going is waited for up to a time limit.
- `scripts/promote.sh` — `die`, `channels.json`, `ci_gate`, `public_gate`, `undo_promote`, `scripts/land.sh`, `fast_lane`, `is not published yet`: points a channel at a published release (main = origin/main, the tag on origin/main and on the public repo, green CI forward), landed by pull request, then a `channels-…` fast-lane tag for the publisher.
- `scripts/channels.sh` — `tjs channels`: the channel page from a checkout.
- `src/taxjson/lib/channels.py` — `PUBLIC_REMOTE`, `fetch`, `read_channels`, `parse_channels`, `release_tags`, `status`, `render`, `dev_checkout`, `check_deployed`: `tjs channels`, the checkout `promote` and `deploy` use, and deploy's version check.
- `src/taxjson/cli/maintainer.py` — `cmd_channels`, `cmd_promote`, `cmd_deploy`, `_run_script`; `src/taxjson/cli/main.py` — `_RELEASE_CMDS`: the release commands.
- `scripts/dev-setup.sh` — `venv/bin/pip install`, `install_hook`, `--hook-only`, `editable_check.py --clean`: developer setup (venv, editable installs, the pre-push hook in the clone's own hooks folder).
- `scripts/editable_check.py` — `problems`, `clean`, `egg_infos`, `PACKAGES`: the venv's taxjson and taxjson-fetch versions match the code's (`dev-setup.sh` runs it; `release.sh` reinstalls both after a version bump); `--clean` first removes the `<name>.egg-info` an editable build leaves beside the sources.

## The CI gate and repository checks

`scripts/ci.sh` is the gate: lint, types (mypy strict on model, rules,
characterize and the engine: `pyproject.toml` `[tool.mypy]`, its overrides table the
engine modules not typed yet, a list `tests/test_type_checks.py` lets only shrink), consistency, tax-rules, PII scan, the
architecture ratchet, the full
suite (core and the fetch plugin, in an empty HOME, offline; the core suite
in parallel), the extras-sensitive tests with the extras hidden, and the
fuzzers. A push must see its result line PASS; a full PASS from a clean tree
is recorded by tree hash for release.sh to reuse. The pre-push hook scans
what a push would publish.

- `scripts/ci.sh` — `stage`, `stage types`, `finish`, `fuzz_run`, `--nightly`, `--serial`, `--release-edits`, `SUITE_ENV`, `NO_PLUGIN`, `stage no-extras`: the gate's stages (the suite with the broker-fetch plugin hidden, as every GitHub job runs it).
- `scripts/run_tests_parallel.py` — `discover_modules`, `plan`, `child_env`, `parse_result`, `skipped_of`, `_COLLECT`, `DURATIONS`, `SPLIT_OVER`: the suite on N processes (a process per module, its own TMPDIR and HOME, longest first, the count checked against discovery and a TestCase class with tests bound where it is not defined refused, read from what each module's suite runs: in a second module (a load_tests's too), under a second name, or a helper's held at its top (a class a factory builds runs where it is found; one a load_tests builds cases from, a doctest's too, fails only on the same test ids from two modules); the `Ran` line names the parts' skipped total, which a batch of test moves compares with its base's).
- `scripts/gate-record.sh` — `gate_dir`, `write`, `find`, `FULL_MODES`: the PASS records by tree hash (`~/.cache/taxjson-gate`).
- `scripts/ci_no_extras.sh` — `EXTRAS_TESTS`, `PLUGIN_TESTS`, `hide_optional.py`, `NONET`, `env -i`, `--list`: the tests that touch an optional extra or the broker-fetch plugin, run as CI's matrix runs the suite (no extras, no taxjson-fetch distribution, empty HOME, no network, in parallel); `--list` prints its modules (`tests/features/repo_checks/test_gate_as_strict_as_ci.py` holds them to the pattern).
- `scripts/hide_optional.py` — `render`, `_HideExtras`, `_without_plugin`, `--extras`, `--plugin`: the sitecustomize that hides the optional extras and/or the broker-fetch plugin (its distribution wherever its metadata lies, its module but from a PYTHONPATH entry); the no-extras stage hides both, the gate's suite stages the plugin.
- `tests/_hermetic/__init__.py` — `install`, `rate_cache`, `SYNTHETIC_RATES`, `RATE_SETS`, `home_env`, `home_vars`, `CacheHome`, `_UnderHome`, `environ`, `ENVIRON`: the synthetic HOME (offline, made-up exchange rates) every test process runs in; `home_env` makes another, at a rate set of `RATE_SETS` (fx4: four places, for the views A/B); `home_vars` gives a HOME's environment entries without writing (tests/_qa_project's ENV takes the setup's own, `HOME`); `environ(...)` is the environment the setup left (`ENVIRON`) with entries added, the base of every environment a helper builds at its import; the finder `CacheHome` runs every import of lib/rate_cache and lib/crypto_prices under the setup's HOME, whatever HOME the environment holds, and the setup imports nothing of taxjson itself. No module of the test trees (the plugin's too) uses `os.environ` directly at its import but to read it (`.get`, `[key]`, `in`), through an alias an import or a plain assignment makes, the setup's `ENVIRON` aside: `tests/test_test_layout.py` — `environ_at_import`, `_at_import`, `environ_leaks`. A function the import calls is not seen: `tests/_tmpfiles.py` — `isolate_layouts` still sets `TAXJSON_LAYOUTS` for the process, and `packages/taxjson-fetch/tests/_bootstrap.py` — `_setup` puts the plugin on `PYTHONPATH`.
- `scripts/check-consistency.sh` — `CHANGELOG`, `channels.json`: versions, CHANGELOG heading and channels agree.
- `scripts/check-pii.sh` — `main`, `report`, `amount_filter`, `sin_filter`, `entropy_filter`, `CRED_RE`, `--diff`, `mask_hit`, `locate_added`, `hide_loc`, `fig_loc`: the personal-data and secret scan (tree, diff, messages; a tree git cannot list fails closed, naming how to pass paths); a hit shows its file and line (a file-name or figure-list hit too), its content masked; `--help` prints the header.
- `scripts/check-public.sh` — `SPLIT_PY`, `PRS_PY`, `gh api --paginate`, `check-pii.sh`, `--days`, `commits`, `reviews`: scans release notes, issues, pull requests, comments, each pull request's reviews (their bodies: `pulls/N/reviews`) and (with `--days`) recent commit messages on GitHub (read-only).
- `.gitleaks.toml` — `useDefault`: CI's gitleaks job's config (`.github/workflows/tests.yml`, job `secrets`); no exceptions, an exception is an exact value per rule and file.
- `scripts/gitleaks-regression.sh` — `rnd`, `WANT_FOUND`, `WANT_QUIET`: proves a gitleaks config still reports ordinary secrets whatever they are called (CI runs the base commit's copy on a pull request).
- `scripts/hooks/pre-push` — `refuse`, `tag_refused`, `check-pii.sh`: the pre-push hook (the tag guard, then the PII scan).
- `scripts/check_architecture.py` — `LAYERS`, `LEAVES`, `LEAF_PARENTS` (modules every layer may import: the rule ids and the country resolver; they and their packages import nothing from taxjson), `MAX_LINES`, `import_graph`, `type_checking_only` (an import under a module-level `if TYPE_CHECKING:` is no edge), `cycles`, `allow_listed_edges`, `layer_violations`, `named_modules`, `plugin_bin_edges`, `OLD_PACKAGES`, `ruff_version`, `ruff_counts`, `ruff_config`, `misplaced_ruff_settings`, `noqa_counts`, `noqa_in_text`, `long_functions`, `money_counts`, `MONEY_WORDS`, `--shrink`, `--accept-growth`: the refactor's ratchet; a count may fall, not rise (`money`: step 6's bare `round(` and money `float(` per file). Its baseline is `scripts/architecture-baseline.json`.
